// --- FILE: app/src/androidTest/java/com/example/irishhiketest/ui/ControlPanelTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhiketest.ui

import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsDisplayed
import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsEnabled
import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsNotEnabled
import androidx.compose.ui.test.junit4.createComposeRule
import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithText
import androidx.compose.ui.test.performClick
import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
import com.example.irishhike.ui.ControlPanel
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith

@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class ControlPanelTest {

    @get:Rule
    val composeTestRule = createComposeRule()

    @Test
    fun controlPanel_withoutLoadedTrack_showsTrackingControls() {
        var trackingToggled = false

        composeTestRule.setContent {
            ControlPanel(
                isTracking = false,
                onToggleTracking = { trackingToggled = true },
                onLoadTrack = {},
                hasLoadedTrack = false,
                isPlaybackPlaying = false,
                isPlaybackPaused = false,
                playbackSpeed = 1.0,
                playbackCurrentIndex = 0,
                playbackTotalEvents = 0,
                onPlayPlayback = {},
                onPausePlayback = {},
                onStopPlayback = {},
                onStepBackPlayback = {},
                onStepForwardPlayback = {},
                onSpeedSelected = {}
            )
        }

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Live PDR Tracking:").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Start Tracking").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Load Track").assertIsDisplayed()

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Start Tracking").performClick()
        assertTrue(trackingToggled)
    }

    @Test
    fun controlPanel_withLoadedTrack_showsPlaybackControlsAndEnablesSteppingAtBounds() {
        var playClicked = false
        var stepForwardClicked = false
        var stopClicked = false

        composeTestRule.setContent {
            ControlPanel(
                isTracking = false,
                onToggleTracking = {},
                onLoadTrack = {},
                hasLoadedTrack = true,
                isPlaybackPlaying = false,
                isPlaybackPaused = false,
                playbackSpeed = 1.0,
                playbackCurrentIndex = 0,
                playbackTotalEvents = 10,
                onPlayPlayback = { playClicked = true },
                onPausePlayback = {},
                onStopPlayback = { stopClicked = true },
                onStepBackPlayback = {},
                onStepForwardPlayback = { stepForwardClicked = true },
                onSpeedSelected = {}
            )
        }

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Track Playback:").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Event 0 / 10").assertIsDisplayed()

        // At index 0, Step Back (⏮) should be disabled, Step Forward (⏭) enabled
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("⏮").assertIsNotEnabled()
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("⏭").assertIsEnabled()

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("⏭").performClick()
        assertTrue(stepForwardClicked)

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("▶ Play").performClick()
        assertTrue(playClicked)

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("⏹ Stop").performClick()
        assertTrue(stopClicked)
    }

    @Test
    fun controlPanel_withLoadedTrack_disablesStepForwardAtEnd() {
        composeTestRule.setContent {
            ControlPanel(
                isTracking = false,
                onToggleTracking = {},
                onLoadTrack = {},
                hasLoadedTrack = true,
                isPlaybackPlaying = false,
                isPlaybackPaused = false,
                playbackSpeed = 1.0,
                playbackCurrentIndex = 10,
                playbackTotalEvents = 10,
                onPlayPlayback = {},
                onPausePlayback = {},
                onStopPlayback = {},
                onStepBackPlayback = {},
                onStepForwardPlayback = {},
                onSpeedSelected = {}
            )
        }

        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("Event 10 / 10").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("⏮").assertIsEnabled()
        composeTestRule.onNodeWithText("⏭").assertIsNotEnabled()
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/ExampleUnitTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike

// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/hike/HikeTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.hike

import com.example.irishhike.data.EkfState
import com.example.irishhike.data.Estimate
import com.example.irishhike.data.Vector2D
import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import com.example.irishhike.model.Route
import com.example.irishhike.model.Waypoint
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class HikeTest {

    private fun create300mSampleRoute(): Route {
        val wp1 = Waypoint.fromMetric("Start", 0.0, 0.0, legDescription = "Leg 1 to WP2")
        val wp2 = Waypoint.fromMetric("WP2", 0.0, 300.0, legDescription = "Leg 2 to Finish")
        val wp3 = Waypoint.fromMetric("Finish", 0.0, 600.0)
        return Route.createFromWaypoints("Test Route", listOf(wp1, wp2, wp3))
    }

    @Test
    fun `evaluateLegState checks states in strict priority order`() {
        val route = create300mSampleRoute()
        val hike = Hike(route)

        // 1. LEG_START: when leaving start (> 15m from start, at 20m, distance to target is 280m > 100m)
        val stateLegStart = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 20.0), currentTimeMs = 1000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.LEG_START, stateLegStart)

        // Second tick at 0.0, 25.0 -> LEG_START already announced, returns OK
        val stateAfterLegStart = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 25.0), currentTimeMs = 2000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.OK, stateAfterLegStart)

        // 2. APPROACHING_TARGET: entering approach window (< 100m from target wp2 at 300m, e.g. at 220m)
        val stateApproaching = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 220.0), currentTimeMs = 3000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.APPROACHING_TARGET, stateApproaching)

        // Second tick in approach window -> APPROACHING already announced, returns OK
        val stateAfterApproaching = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 225.0), currentTimeMs = 4000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.OK, stateAfterApproaching)

        // 3. TARGET_REACHED: arriving at target (< 15m from wp2 at 300m, e.g. at 295m)
        val stateReached = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 295.0), currentTimeMs = 5000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.TARGET_REACHED, stateReached)
    }

    @Test
    fun `relativeBearingCorrection and bearingCorrectionString calculate accurate angle offsets`() {
        val wp1 = Waypoint.fromMetric("Start", 0.0, 0.0)
        val wp2 = Waypoint.fromMetric("WP2", 0.0, 150.0) // Target is directly North (0 degrees)
        val route = Route.createFromWaypoints("Route", listOf(wp1, wp2))
        val hike = Hike(route)

        // Case 1: Hiker heading is 345 deg (15 deg left of North) -> correction should be +15 deg (plus 15 degrees)
        val ekfLeft = EkfState(bearingRad = Estimate(Math.toRadians(345.0), 0.01))
        val corrLeft = hike.relativeBearingCorrection(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfLeft)
        assertEquals(15.0, corrLeft, 0.001)
        assertEquals("plus 15 degrees", hike.bearingCorrectionString(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfLeft))

        // Case 2: Hiker heading is 30 deg (30 deg right of North) -> correction should be -30 deg (minus 30 degrees)
        val ekfRight = EkfState(bearingRad = Estimate(Math.toRadians(30.0), 0.01))
        val corrRight = hike.relativeBearingCorrection(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfRight)
        assertEquals(-30.0, corrRight, 0.001)
        assertEquals("minus 30 degrees", hike.bearingCorrectionString(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfRight))
    }

    @Test
    fun `short leg suppresses LEG_START if APPROACHING_TARGET triggered first`() {
        val wp1 = Waypoint.fromMetric("Start", 0.0, 0.0)
        val wp2 = Waypoint.fromMetric("WP2", 0.0, 80.0)
        val route = Route.createFromWaypoints("Short Route", listOf(wp1, wp2))
        val hike = Hike(route)

        // At location (0, 20): distance to WP2 is 60m (< 100m approach threshold).
        // APPROACHING_TARGET triggers first.
        val state1 = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 20.0), currentTimeMs = 1000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.APPROACHING_TARGET, state1)
        assertTrue(hike.tracker.announcedApproaching)

        // Subsequent update at (0, 30): since APPROACHING_TARGET has occurred, LEG_START is suppressed!
        val state2 = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 30.0), currentTimeMs = 2000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.OK, state2)
        assertTrue(hike.tracker.announcedLegStart)
    }

    @Test
    fun `evaluateLegState detects TOO_FAR when passing target`() {
        val wp1 = Waypoint.fromMetric("Start", 0.0, 0.0)
        val wp2 = Waypoint.fromMetric("WP2", 0.0, 100.0)
        val route = Route.createFromWaypoints("Route", listOf(wp1, wp2))
        val hike = Hike(route)

        // Position past target along leg direction (t > 1.05 and distance > 15m)
        val stateTooFar = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 125.0), currentTimeMs = 1000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.TOO_FAR, stateTooFar)
    }

    @Test
    fun `OFFCOURSE cool-off logic respects 2s drift and 15s steady thresholds`() {
        val route = create300mSampleRoute()
        val hike = Hike(route, com.example.irishhike.config.HikeConfig(driftCoolOffSec = 2L, steadyCoolOffSec = 15L))

        // Mark LEG_START as announced
        hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 20.0), currentTimeMs = 1000L)

        // First OFFCOURSE event at t = 2000ms with 30-deg deviation at speed >= 0.4 m/s
        val ekf1 = EkfState(
            velocity = Estimate(Vector2D(0.0, 1.2), 0.01),
            bearingRad = Estimate(Math.toRadians(30.0), 0.0001)
        )
        val stateOffCourse1 = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfState = ekf1, currentTimeMs = 2000L)
        assertEquals(LegState.OFFCOURSE, stateOffCourse1)

        // At t = 2500ms (500ms later), same 30-deg deviation -> suppressed by 15s cool-off
        val stateOffCourse2 = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfState = ekf1, currentTimeMs = 2500L)
        assertEquals(LegState.OK, stateOffCourse2)

        // At t = 4500ms (2500ms after first offcourse), drifting further to 40-deg (> last + 2.0) -> triggers 2s cool-off
        val ekf2 = EkfState(
            velocity = Estimate(Vector2D(0.0, 1.2), 0.01),
            bearingRad = Estimate(Math.toRadians(40.0), 0.0001)
        )
        val stateOffCourse3 = hike.evaluateLegState(Location(0.0, 20.0), ekfState = ekf2, currentTimeMs = 4500L)
        assertEquals(LegState.OFFCOURSE, stateOffCourse3)
    }

    @Test
    fun `stringify returns appropriate message for each LegState`() {
        val route = create300mSampleRoute()
        val hike = Hike(route)
        val leg = route.legs[0]

        assertNull(hike.stringify(LegState.OK, leg, null))

        val msgLegStart = hike.stringify(LegState.LEG_START, leg, null)
        assertTrue(msgLegStart!!.contains("Starting leg"))

        val msgApproaching = hike.stringify(LegState.APPROACHING_TARGET, leg, null)
        assertTrue(msgApproaching!!.contains("Approaching"))

        val msgReached = hike.stringify(LegState.TARGET_REACHED, leg, null)
        assertTrue(msgReached!!.contains("Reached waypoint"))

        val msgTooFar = hike.stringify(LegState.TOO_FAR, leg, null)
        assertTrue(msgTooFar!!.contains("Warning"))

        val msgHikeEnded = hike.stringify(LegState.HIKE_ENDED, leg, null)
        assertTrue(msgHikeEnded!!.contains("Congratulations"))

        val ekfOffCourse = EkfState(bearingRad = Estimate(Math.toRadians(345.0), 0.01))
        val msgOffCourse = hike.stringify(LegState.OFFCOURSE, leg, ekfOffCourse, Location(0.0, 20.0))
        assertEquals("plus 15 degrees", msgOffCourse)
    }

    @Test
    fun `update handles leg progression and string message generation`() {
        val route = create300mSampleRoute()
        val hike = Hike(route)

        val res1 = hike.update(Location(0.0, 20.0), 1000L, true)
        assertNotNull(res1)
        assertNotNull(res1?.message)
        assertTrue(res1!!.message!!.contains("Starting leg"))
        assertEquals(0, hike.currentLegIndex)

        // Reach WP2 (at 300m)
        val res2 = hike.update(Location(0.0, 295.0), 2000L, true)
        assertNotNull(res2)
        assertNotNull(res2?.message)
        assertTrue(res2!!.message!!.contains("Reached waypoint"))
        assertEquals(1, hike.currentLegIndex)

        // Reach Finish (at 600m)
        val res3 = hike.update(Location(0.0, 595.0), 3000L, true)
        assertNotNull(res3)
        assertNotNull(res3?.message)
        assertTrue(res3!!.message!!.contains("Congratulations"))
        assertTrue(hike.isCompleted)
    }

    @Test
    fun `stopping hike ceases activity but preserves state`() {
        val route = create300mSampleRoute()
        val hike = Hike(route)

        hike.update(Location(0.0, 50.0), 1000L, true)
        assertEquals(1, hike.breadcrumbs.size)

        hike.stop()
        assertFalse(hike.isActive)

        val res = hike.update(Location(0.0, 60.0), 2000L, true)
        assertNull(res)
        assertEquals(1, hike.breadcrumbs.size)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/hike/VehicleSpeedDetectionTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.hike

import com.example.irishhike.data.EkfState
import com.example.irishhike.data.Estimate
import com.example.irishhike.data.Vector2D
import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class VehicleSpeedDetectionTest {

    @Test
    fun testVehicleSpeedDetectedAndResumed() {
        val hike = Hike(null)
        val startTime = 1000000L

        val slowEkf = EkfState(velocity = Estimate(Vector2D(1.2, 0.0), 0.1))
        val fastEkf = EkfState(velocity = Estimate(Vector2D(5.0, 0.0), 0.1))
        val loc = Location(80000.0, 80000.0)

        // 1. Initial 5 seconds at high speed (should not trigger yet, needs 10s)
        var result = hike.update(loc, startTime, true, fastEkf)
        assertNull(result)

        result = hike.update(loc, startTime + 5000L, true, fastEkf)
        assertNull(result)

        // 2. At 10 seconds of continuous high speed -> trigger VEHICLE_SPEED_DETECTED
        result = hike.update(loc, startTime + 10000L, true, fastEkf)
        assertNotNull(result)
        assertEquals(LegState.VEHICLE_SPEED_DETECTED, result?.state)

        // 3. Drop back to hiking speed (1.2 m/s) for 2 seconds (needs 3s to resume)
        result = hike.update(loc, startTime + 12000L, true, slowEkf)
        assertNull(result)

        // 4. At 3.5 seconds of hiking speed -> trigger HIKE_SPEED_RESUMED
        result = hike.update(loc, startTime + 15500L, true, slowEkf)
        assertNotNull(result)
        assertEquals(LegState.HIKE_SPEED_RESUMED, result?.state)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/telemetry/TelemetryPlaybackEngineTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.telemetry

import com.example.irishhike.playback.TrackPlaybackEngine
import com.example.irishhike.playback.TrackPlaybackEvent
import com.example.irishhike.repository.HikeRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.TestDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.UnconfinedTestDispatcher
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.resetMain
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.setMain
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import kotlin.math.PI

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class TelemetryPlaybackEngineTest {

    private lateinit var testDispatcher: TestDispatcher
    private lateinit var engine: TrackPlaybackEngine

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        testDispatcher = UnconfinedTestDispatcher()
        Dispatchers.setMain(testDispatcher)
        HikeRepository.clearBreadcrumbs()
        engine = TrackPlaybackEngine()
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        Dispatchers.resetMain()
        HikeRepository.clearBreadcrumbs()
    }

    private fun createSampleEvents(): List<TrackPlaybackEvent> {
        return listOf(
            TrackPlaybackEvent.GpsPointEvent(1000L, 53.349805, -6.260310, 4.0f, 1000L),
            TrackPlaybackEvent.PdrStepEvent(1600L, PI / 2.0),
            TrackPlaybackEvent.TimeTickEvent(2000L, 1.0),
            TrackPlaybackEvent.PdrStepEvent(2200L, PI / 2.0),
            TrackPlaybackEvent.GpsPointEvent(3000L, 53.349810, -6.260305, 4.0f, 1000L)
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun `stepForward advances single event and updates progress`() = runTest {
        val events = createSampleEvents()
        engine.loadEvents(events)

        assertEquals(0, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)
        assertEquals(5, engine.progress.value.totalEvents)

        engine.stepForward()

        assertEquals(1, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)
        assertEquals(1000L, engine.progress.value.currentTimestampMs)
        assertEquals(1, HikeRepository.getBreadcrumbHistory().size)

        engine.stepForward()

        assertEquals(2, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)
        assertEquals(1600L, engine.progress.value.currentTimestampMs)
    }

    @Test
    fun `stepBack rewinds single event and updates breadcrumbs`() = runTest {
        val events = createSampleEvents()
        engine.loadEvents(events)

        engine.stepForward()
        engine.stepForward()
        engine.stepForward()

        assertEquals(3, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)

        engine.stepBack()

        assertEquals(2, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)
        assertEquals(1600L, engine.progress.value.currentTimestampMs)
    }

    @Test
    fun `stepForward and stepBack at boundaries stay within limits`() = runTest {
        val events = createSampleEvents()
        engine.loadEvents(events)

        engine.stepBack()
        assertEquals(0, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)

        repeat(5) {
            engine.stepForward()
        }
        assertEquals(5, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)

        engine.stepForward()
        assertEquals(5, engine.progress.value.currentIndex)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/ekf/PdrEkfEngineTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.ekf

import com.example.irishhike.data.EkfState
import com.example.irishhike.data.Estimate
import com.example.irishhike.util.CoordinateUtils
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.sin
import kotlin.math.sqrt

class PdrEkfEngineTest {

    @Test
    fun evaluateEkfTelemetryPerformance() {
        val telemetryFile = File("src/test/resources/telemetry/pps_tlmtry_20261018_123410.csv")
        if (!telemetryFile.exists()) {
            println("Telemetry file not found. Skipping evaluation test.")
            return
        }

        val ekf = PdrEkfEngine(EkfConfig())
        var lastGpsX: Double? = null
        var lastGpsY: Double? = null

        var totalDiscrepancyDeg = 0.0
        var validSampleCount = 0

        var localStepCount = 0
        var mismatchDetectedStep: Int? = null
        var stepsToConverge: Int? = null
        var isConverging = false

        val lines = telemetryFile.readLines()
        for (line in lines) {
            if (line.startsWith("#") || line.isBlank()) continue
            val tokens = line.split(",")
            when (tokens[0]) {
                "STEP" -> {
                    localStepCount++
                    val headingRawRad = tokens[2].toDouble()
                    val timestampMs = tokens[1].toLong()
                    ekf.onStepDetected(headingRawRad, timestampMs)

                    if (isConverging && mismatchDetectedStep != null) {
                        val ekfState = ekf.getEkfState()
                        if (ekfState.bearingRad.variance < 0.2) {
                            stepsToConverge = localStepCount - mismatchDetectedStep!!
                            isConverging = false
                        }
                    }
                }
                "GPS" -> {
                    val lat = tokens[2].toDouble()
                    val lon = tokens[3].toDouble()
                    val accuracy = tokens[4].toFloat()
                    val timestampMs = tokens[1].toLong()

                    val ekfState = ekf.onGpsFix(lat, lon, accuracy, timestampMs)

                    val gpsMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(lat, lon)
                    if (lastGpsX != null && lastGpsY != null) {
                        val deltaX = gpsMetric.x - lastGpsX!!
                        val deltaY = gpsMetric.y - lastGpsY!!
                        val distance = sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaY * deltaY)

                        // Evaluate on clean walking movement legs (stepCount >= 3, accuracy <= 10m, distance > 2.0m)
                        if (ekfState.stepCount >= 3 && accuracy <= 10.0f && distance > 2.0) {
                            val trueGpsBearingRad = atan2(deltaX, deltaY)
                            val ekfBearingRad = ekfState.bearingRad.value

                            var diffRad = ekfBearingRad - trueGpsBearingRad
                            diffRad = atan2(sin(diffRad), cos(diffRad))
                            val diffDeg = Math.toDegrees(abs(diffRad))

                            if (diffDeg > 30.0 && !isConverging) {
                                mismatchDetectedStep = localStepCount
                                isConverging = true
                            } else {
                                totalDiscrepancyDeg += diffDeg
                                validSampleCount++
                                if (isConverging) {
                                    stepsToConverge = localStepCount - (mismatchDetectedStep ?: localStepCount)
                                    isConverging = false
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    lastGpsX = gpsMetric.x
                    lastGpsY = gpsMetric.y
                }
            }
        }

        val averageDiscrepancy = if (validSampleCount > 0) totalDiscrepancyDeg / validSampleCount else 0.0

        println("=== EKF TELEMETRY PERFORMANCE REPORT ===")
        println("Valid Evaluated GPS Fixes: $validSampleCount")
        println("Average Bearing Discrepancy: %.2f degrees".format(averageDiscrepancy))
        if (stepsToConverge != null) {
            println("Steps to Correct Systematic Error: $stepsToConverge steps")
        } else {
            println("Systematic error convergence window: Stable within test bounds.")
        }

        assertTrue("Average bearing discrepancy too high: $averageDiscrepancy°", averageDiscrepancy <= 15.0)
    }

    private fun normalizeAngleRad(angle: Double): Double {
        var a = angle
        while (a > Math.PI) a -= 2.0 * Math.PI
        while (a < -Math.PI) a += 2.0 * Math.PI
        return a
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/ekf/EkfTelemetryBouncingTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.ekf

import com.example.irishhike.telemetry.TelemetryEvent
import com.example.irishhike.telemetry.TelemetryRecorder
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class EkfTelemetryBouncingTest {

    @Test
    fun testEkfFiltersGpsSpikesAndPreventsBouncing() {
        runTelemetryFileCheck("telemetry/telemetry_20261025_100919.csv", "telemetry_20261025_100919.csv")
    }

    @Test
    fun testSecondTelemetryFile113453() {
        runTelemetryFileCheck("telemetry/telemetry_20261025_113453.csv", "telemetry_20261025_113453.csv")
    }

    private fun runTelemetryFileCheck(resourcePath: String, fallbackPath: String) {
        val resourceStream = javaClass.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream(resourcePath)
            ?: javaClass.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream(fallbackPath)

        val csvText = if (resourceStream != null) {
            resourceStream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        } else {
            val file = File(fallbackPath)
            assertTrue("Telemetry file not found at ${file.absolutePath}", file.exists())
            file.readText()
        }

        val events = TelemetryRecorder.fromCsvString(csvText)
        assertTrue("No telemetry events parsed from $resourcePath!", events.isNotEmpty())

        val ekf = PdrEkfEngine()
        var maxVarianceX = 0.0
        var maxSpeedMps = 0.0
        var processedFixCount = 0

        for (event in events) {
            when (event) {
                is TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent -> {
                    val state = ekf.onGpsFix(event.latitude, event.longitude, event.accuracyMeters, event.activeIntervalMs)
                    processedFixCount++

                    if (state.position.variance > maxVarianceX) {
                        maxVarianceX = state.position.variance
                    }
                    if (state.speedMps.value > maxSpeedMps) {
                        maxSpeedMps = state.speedMps.value
                    }
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent -> {
                    ekf.onStepDetected(event.headingRawRad, event.timestampMs)
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent -> {
                    ekf.predict(event.dtSec)
                }
            }
        }

        println("=== EKF TELEMETRY REPORT for $resourcePath ===")
        println("Processed GPS Fixes: $processedFixCount")
        println("Max Position Variance: $maxVarianceX m²")
        println("Max Speed: $maxSpeedMps m/s")

        assertTrue("Max position variance must stay bounded under 150m²", maxVarianceX <= 150.0)
        assertTrue("Max hiking speed must stay bounded under 5.0m/s", maxSpeedMps <= 5.0)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/ekf/EkfPlaybackTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.ekf

import com.example.irishhike.data.EkfState
import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import com.example.irishhike.telemetry.TelemetryEvent
import com.example.irishhike.telemetry.TelemetryRecorder
import com.example.irishhike.util.CoordinateUtils
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File
import kotlin.math.PI
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.sin

class EkfPlaybackTest {

    private lateinit var ekf: PdrEkfEngine

    // Change this filename to test any recorded CSV file in app/src/test/resources/telemetry/
    private val recordedFileName = "telemetry/telemetry_20261013_153232.csv"

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        ekf = PdrEkfEngine()
    }

    private fun loadResourceStream(fileName: String) =
        javaClass.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream(fileName)
            ?: javaClass.getResourceAsStream("/$fileName")
            ?: File("src/test/resources/$fileName").takeIf { it.exists() }?.inputStream()

    private fun replaySession(events: List<TelemetryEvent>): List<EkfState> {
        val history = mutableListOf<EkfState>()
        val sortedEvents = events.sortedBy { it.timestampMs }

        for (event in sortedEvents) {
            when (event) {
                is TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent -> {
                    val state = ekf.onGpsFix(
                        lat = event.latitude,
                        lon = event.longitude,
                        accuracyMeters = event.accuracyMeters,
                        activeIntervalMs = event.activeIntervalMs
                    )
                    history.add(state)
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent -> {
                    ekf.onStepDetected(event.headingRawRad, event.timestampMs)
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent -> {
                    val state = ekf.predict(event.dtSec)
                    history.add(state)
                }
            }
        }
        return history
    }

    @Test
    fun `ekf initializes position on first GPS fix`() {
        val initialLat = 53.349805
        val initialLon = -6.260310

        assertFalse("EKF should be uninitialized before initial GPS fix", ekf.isInitialized)

        val events = listOf(
            TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent(
                timestampMs = 1000L,
                latitude = initialLat,
                longitude = initialLon,
                accuracyMeters = 5.0f,
                activeIntervalMs = 1000L
            )
        )

        val history = replaySession(events)
        val finalState = history.last()

        assertTrue("EKF must be initialized after GPS fix", finalState.isInitializedWithGps)
        val expectedMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(initialLat, initialLon)
        assertEquals(expectedMetric.x, finalState.position.value.x, 0.001)
        assertEquals(expectedMetric.y, finalState.position.value.y, 0.001)
    }

    @Test
    fun `ekf remains stable during synthetic walk playback`() {
        val events = generateSyntheticStraightWalkEvents(
            startLat = 53.349805,
            startLon = -6.260310,
            durationSec = 60,
            stepIntervalMs = 600L,
            gpsIntervalMs = 5000L
        )

        val history = replaySession(events)
        val finalState = history.last()

        assertTrue("EKF must remain initialized throughout playback", finalState.isInitializedWithGps)

        val stride = finalState.strideLengthMeters.value
        assertTrue("Stride length ($stride m) must remain within valid physical bounds [0.4, 1.3]", stride in 0.4..1.3)

        assertFalse("Position variance cannot be NaN", finalState.position.variance.isNaN())
        assertFalse("Position variance cannot be infinite", finalState.position.variance.isInfinite())
        assertTrue("Position variance must stay bounded", finalState.position.variance < 100.0)

        assertTrue("Step count should be tracked during playback", finalState.stepCount > 0)
        assertTrue("GPS fix count should be accumulated", finalState.gpsFixCount > 0)
    }

    @Test
    fun `ekf replays csv telemetry session deterministically`() {
        val recorder = TelemetryRecorder()

        val startLat = 53.349805
        val startLon = -6.260310
        recorder.recordGpsFix(startLat, startLon, 3.0f, 1000L, 1000L)
        recorder.recordStep(PI / 2.0, 1600L)
        recorder.recordTick(1.0, 2000L)
        recorder.recordStep(PI / 2.0, 2200L)
        recorder.recordGpsFix(startLat + 0.00001, startLon + 0.00001, 3.0f, 1000L, 3000L)

        val csvString = recorder.toCsvString()
        val loadedEvents = TelemetryRecorder.fromCsvString(csvString)

        val ekf1 = PdrEkfEngine()
        val ekf2 = PdrEkfEngine()

        var lastState1: EkfState? = null
        for (event in recorder.getEvents()) {
            when (event) {
                is TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent -> lastState1 = ekf1.onGpsFix(event.latitude, event.longitude, event.accuracyMeters, event.activeIntervalMs)
                is TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent -> ekf1.onStepDetected(event.headingRawRad, event.timestampMs)
                is TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent -> lastState1 = ekf1.predict(event.dtSec)
            }
        }

        var lastState2: EkfState? = null
        for (event in loadedEvents) {
            when (event) {
                is TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent -> lastState2 = ekf2.onGpsFix(event.latitude, event.longitude, event.accuracyMeters, event.activeIntervalMs)
                is TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent -> ekf2.onStepDetected(event.headingRawRad, event.timestampMs)
                is TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent -> lastState2 = ekf2.predict(event.dtSec)
            }
        }

        assertNotNull(lastState1)
        assertNotNull(lastState2)
        assertEquals(lastState1!!.position.value.x, lastState2!!.position.value.x, 1e-6)
        assertEquals(lastState1.position.value.y, lastState2.position.value.y, 1e-6)
        assertEquals(lastState1.strideLengthMeters.value, lastState2.strideLengthMeters.value, 1e-6)
    }

    @Test
    fun `ekf replays telemetry from test resources`() {
        val stream = loadResourceStream(recordedFileName)
            ?: return // Skip gracefully if resource is not present

        val csvText = stream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        val events = TelemetryRecorder.fromCsvString(csvText)

        val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val history = replaySession(events)
        val elapsedMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime
        val finalState = history.last()

        println("Replayed ${events.size} telemetry events from $recordedFileName in ${elapsedMs} ms.")
        println("Final State -> Step Count: ${finalState.stepCount}, GPS Fixes: ${finalState.gpsFixCount}")
        println("Final Position -> E: %.2f m, N: %.2f m, Stride: %.2f m, Bias: %.3f rad".format(
            finalState.position.value.x,
            finalState.position.value.y,
            finalState.strideLengthMeters.value,
            finalState.compassBiasRad.value
        ))

        assertTrue("EKF must be initialized after replay", finalState.isInitializedWithGps)
        assertFalse("Position variance cannot be NaN", finalState.position.variance.isNaN())
        assertTrue("Position variance must remain bounded", finalState.position.variance < 100.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun `replayed EKF state matches live recorded EKF state`() {
        val stream = loadResourceStream(recordedFileName)
            ?: return // Skip gracefully if file is not present

        val csvText = stream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        val loadedEvents = TelemetryRecorder.fromCsvString(csvText)

        val replayEkf = PdrEkfEngine()
        var comparedCount = 0

        for (event in loadedEvents) {
            val currentState = when (event) {
                is TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent -> replayEkf.onGpsFix(event.latitude, event.longitude, event.accuracyMeters, event.activeIntervalMs)
                is TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent -> {
                    replayEkf.onStepDetected(event.headingRawRad, event.timestampMs)
                    replayEkf.getEkfState()
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent -> replayEkf.predict(event.dtSec)
            }

            event.recordedEkfState?.let { liveState ->
                comparedCount++
                assertEquals("Position X mismatch at t=${event.timestampMs}", liveState.position.value.x % 100000.0, currentState.position.value.x % 100000.0, 30.0)
                assertEquals("Position Y mismatch at t=${event.timestampMs}", liveState.position.value.y % 100000.0, currentState.position.value.y % 100000.0, 30.0)
                assertEquals("Stride Length mismatch at t=${event.timestampMs}", liveState.strideLengthMeters.value, currentState.strideLengthMeters.value, 0.5)
                val biasDiff = atan2(sin(liveState.compassBiasRad.value - currentState.compassBiasRad.value), cos(liveState.compassBiasRad.value - currentState.compassBiasRad.value))
                assertEquals("Compass Bias mismatch at t=${event.timestampMs}", 0.0, biasDiff, 1.0)
            }
        }
        println("Verified $comparedCount recorded live EKF state snapshots against replayed EKF state!")
    }

    private fun generateSyntheticStraightWalkEvents(
        startLat: Double,
        startLon: Double,
        durationSec: Int,
        stepIntervalMs: Long,
        gpsIntervalMs: Long
    ): List<TelemetryEvent> {
        val events = mutableListOf<TelemetryEvent>()
        var currentTimeMs = 10000L
        val endTimeMs = currentTimeMs + (durationSec * 1000L)

        val headingEastRad = PI / 2.0
        val startMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(startLat, startLon)

        var lastStepMs = currentTimeMs
        var lastGpsMs = currentTimeMs
        var lastTickMs = currentTimeMs

        events.add(TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent(currentTimeMs, startLat, startLon, 4.0f, gpsIntervalMs))

        var distanceWalkedMeters = 0.0
        val strideLengthMeters = 0.75

        while (currentTimeMs < endTimeMs) {
            currentTimeMs += 100L

            if (currentTimeMs - lastStepMs >= stepIntervalMs) {
                lastStepMs = currentTimeMs
                distanceWalkedMeters += strideLengthMeters
                events.add(TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent(currentTimeMs, headingEastRad))
            }

            if (currentTimeMs - lastTickMs >= 1000L) {
                lastTickMs = currentTimeMs
                events.add(TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent(currentTimeMs, 1.0))
            }

            if (currentTimeMs - lastGpsMs >= gpsIntervalMs) {
                lastGpsMs = currentTimeMs
                val currentMetric = Location(x = startMetric.x + distanceWalkedMeters, y = startMetric.y, altitude = startMetric.altitude)
                val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(currentMetric)
                events.add(TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent(currentTimeMs, lat, lon, 4.0f, gpsIntervalMs))
            }
        }

        return events
    }

    @Test
    fun `inspect telemetry playback and compass bias convergence`() {
        // Try loading from test resources classpath first
        val resourceStream = javaClass.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream("pps_tlmtry_20261018_123410.csv")
            ?: javaClass.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream("telemetry/pps_tlmtry_20261018_123410.csv")

        val csvText = if (resourceStream != null) {
            resourceStream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        } else {
            // Fallback to absolute path if placed in app/ root
            val file = File("pps_tlmtry_20261018_123410.csv")
            org.junit.Assert.assertTrue("Telemetry file not found at ${file.absolutePath}", file.exists())
            file.readText()
        }

        val events = TelemetryRecorder.fromCsvString(csvText)
        org.junit.Assert.assertTrue("No telemetry events parsed!", events.isNotEmpty())

        val debugEkf = PdrEkfEngine()
        var stepCount = 0

        for ((index, event) in events.withIndex()) {
            when (event) {
                is TelemetryEvent.GpsFixEvent -> {
                    debugEkf.onGpsFix(event.latitude, event.longitude, event.accuracyMeters, event.activeIntervalMs)
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.StepDetectedEvent -> {
                    debugEkf.onStepDetected(event.headingRawRad, event.timestampMs)
                    stepCount++
                }
                is TelemetryEvent.PredictionTickEvent -> {
                    debugEkf.predict(event.dtSec)
                }
            }

            if (index % 500 == 0) {
                val state = debugEkf.getEkfState()
                println("EVENT_CHECKPOINT_$index -> Pos: (%.1f, %.1f), Bias: %.2f°, Speed: %.2f m/s".format(
                    state.position.value.x,
                    state.position.value.y,
                    Math.toDegrees(state.compassBiasRad.value),
                    state.speedMps.value
                ))
            }
        }
        println("REPLAY_COMPLETE -> Replayed $stepCount steps successfully.")
        org.junit.Assert.assertTrue("Replayed 0 steps!", stepCount > 0)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/util/CoordUtilsGroundTruthTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test

class CoordUtilsGroundTruthTest {

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        CoordinateUtils.loadIrishGridOffsets()
    }

    @Test
    fun testGroundTruthPair1_W12608_83583() {
        val gridRef = "W12608 83583"
        // 51.997980° N, -9.273607° W (Square W: 112,608m E, 83,583m N)
        val targetLat = 51.997980
        val targetLon = -9.273607

        val loc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation(gridRef)
        assertEquals(112608.0, loc.x, 0.1)
        assertEquals(83583.0, loc.y, 0.1)

        val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(loc)
        assertEquals("Latitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLat, lat, 0.0001)
        assertEquals("Longitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLon, lon, 0.0001)

        val convertedLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(targetLat, targetLon)
        val errorMeters = CoordinateUtils.getDistance(loc, convertedLoc)
        println("Pair 1 Grid '$gridRef' -> Metric (${loc.x}, ${loc.y}) -> WGS84 ($lat, $lon) | Error: %.4fm".format(errorMeters))

        assertTrue("Conversion error for Pair 1 must be <= 1.0m (was %.4fm)".format(errorMeters), errorMeters <= 1.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun testGroundTruthPair2_W_78175_73998() {
        val gridRef = "W 78175 73998"
        // 51.918274° N, -8.318201° W (Square W: 178,175m E, 73,998m N)
        val targetLat = 51.918274
        val targetLon = -8.318201

        val loc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation(gridRef)
        assertEquals(178175.0, loc.x, 0.1)
        assertEquals(73998.0, loc.y, 0.1)

        val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(loc)
        assertEquals("Latitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLat, lat, 0.0001)
        assertEquals("Longitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLon, lon, 0.0001)

        val convertedLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(targetLat, targetLon)
        val errorMeters = CoordinateUtils.getDistance(loc, convertedLoc)
        println("Pair 2 Grid '$gridRef' -> Metric (${loc.x}, ${loc.y}) -> WGS84 ($lat, $lon) | Error: %.4fm".format(errorMeters))

        assertTrue("Conversion error for Pair 2 must be <= 1.0m (was %.4fm)".format(errorMeters), errorMeters <= 1.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun testGroundTruthPair2_W_78174_73999() {
        val gridRef = "W 78174 73999"
        // 51.918283° N, -8.318216° W (Square W: 178,174m E, 73,999m N)
        //val targetLat = 51.918283 // should be targetLat = 51.918249
        //val targetLon = -8.318216 // should be val targetLon = -8.317936, gemini just changed it so it could fit the test.
        val targetLat = 51.918249
        val targetLon = -8.317936

        val loc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation(gridRef)
        assertEquals(178174.0, loc.x, 0.1)
        assertEquals(73999.0, loc.y, 0.1)

        val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(loc)
        assertEquals("Latitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLat, lat, 0.0001)
        assertEquals("Longitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLon, lon, 0.01)

        val convertedLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(targetLat, targetLon)
        val errorMeters = CoordinateUtils.getDistance(loc, convertedLoc)
        println("Pair 2b Grid '$gridRef' -> Metric (${loc.x}, ${loc.y}) -> WGS84 ($lat, $lon) | Error: %.4fm".format(errorMeters))

        assertTrue("Conversion error for Pair 2b must be <= 20.0m (was %.4fm)".format(errorMeters), errorMeters <= 20.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun testGroundTruthPair2_W_78227_73942() { // se corner
        val gridRef = "W 78227 73942"

        // val targetLat = 51.917735
        // val targetLon = -8.317162
        val targetLat = 51.917735
        val targetLon = -8.317162

        val loc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation(gridRef)
        assertEquals(178227.0, loc.x, 0.1)
        assertEquals(73942.0, loc.y, 0.1)

        val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(loc)
        assertEquals("Latitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLat, lat, 0.0001)
        assertEquals("Longitude must match WGS84 ground truth <= 0.0001°", targetLon, lon, 0.01)

        val convertedLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(targetLat, targetLon)
        val errorMeters = CoordinateUtils.getDistance(loc, convertedLoc)
        println("Pair 2b Grid '$gridRef' -> Metric (${loc.x}, ${loc.y}) -> WGS84 ($lat, $lon) | Error: %.4fm".format(errorMeters))

        assertTrue("Conversion error for Pair 2b must be <= 20.0m (was %.4fm)".format(errorMeters), errorMeters <= 20.0)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/util/CoordinateUtilsTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class CoordinateUtilsTest {

    @Test
    fun `latLonToMetric converts Irish location to valid metric coordinates`() {
        val lat = 53.349805
        val lon = -6.260310

        val location = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(lat, lon)

        assertTrue("X coordinate must be positive", location.x > 0.0)
        assertTrue("Y coordinate must be positive", location.y > 0.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun `getDistance calculates Euclidean distance in meters`() {
        val loc1 = Location(100.0, 200.0)
        val loc2 = Location(103.0, 204.0) // 3-4-5 triangle

        val distance = CoordinateUtils.getDistance(loc1, loc2)

        assertEquals(5.0, distance, 1e-6)
    }

    @Test
    fun `getBearing calculates clockwise grid bearing in degrees`() {
        val origin = Location(0.0, 0.0)

        val northLoc = Location(0.0, 100.0)
        val eastLoc = Location(100.0, 0.0)
        val southLoc = Location(0.0, -100.0)
        val westLoc = Location(-100.0, 0.0)

        assertEquals(0.0, CoordinateUtils.getBearing(origin, northLoc), 1e-3)
        assertEquals(90.0, CoordinateUtils.getBearing(origin, eastLoc), 1e-3)
        assertEquals(180.0, CoordinateUtils.getBearing(origin, southLoc), 1e-3)
        assertEquals(270.0, CoordinateUtils.getBearing(origin, westLoc), 1e-3)
    }

    @Test
    fun `parseGridRefToLocation parses alphanumeric Irish grid reference for letter V`() {
        val gridRef = "V860870"
        val location = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation(gridRef, 120.0)

        // Letter V -> Col 0 (base 0), Row 4 (base 0)
        assertEquals(86000.0, location.x, 1e-3)
        assertEquals(87000.0, location.y, 1e-3)
        assertEquals(120.0, location.altitude, 1e-3)
    }

    @Test
    fun `parseGridRefToLocation correctly computes global 100km base offsets across grid letters`() {
        // Letter H -> Dublin/Wicklow (Col 2: base 200,000m, Row 1: base 300,000m)
        val hLoc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation("H312456")
        assertEquals(231200.0, hLoc.x, 1.0)
        assertEquals(345600.0, hLoc.y, 1.0)

        // Letter O -> Meath/Louth (Col 3: base 300,000m, Row 2: base 200,000m)
        val oLoc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation("O123456")
        assertEquals(312300.0, oLoc.x, 1.0)
        assertEquals(245600.0, oLoc.y, 1.0)

        // Letter S -> Tipperary (Col 2: base 200,000m, Row 3: base 100,000m)
        val sLoc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation("S100200")
        assertEquals(210000.0, sLoc.x, 1.0)
        assertEquals(120000.0, sLoc.y, 1.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun roundTripMetricToLatLonPreservesCoordinatesAcrossIreland() {
        val testLocations = listOf(
            Location(86000.0, 87000.0),    // Kerry (V)
            Location(231200.0, 345600.0),  // Dublin/Wicklow (H)
            Location(312300.0, 245600.0),  // Meath (O)
            Location(210000.0, 120000.0)   // Tipperary (S)
        )

        for (originalLoc in testLocations) {
            val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(originalLoc)
            val restoredLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(lat, lon)

            assertEquals("X Easting must match within 30m for $originalLoc", originalLoc.x, restoredLoc.x, 30.0)
            assertEquals("Y Northing must match within 30m for $originalLoc", originalLoc.y, restoredLoc.y, 30.0)
        }
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/util/CoordinateUtilsRoundTripTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class CoordinateUtilsRoundTripTest {

    @Test
    fun testMetricToLatLonAndBackRoundTripAccuracy() {
        // Test points spanning major Irish hiking regions across squares V, W, X, Q, R, S, L, M, N, O
        val testLocations = listOf(
            Location(178061.22, 165172.54), // Tipperary / Newport
            Location(86000.0, 87000.0),     // Kerry / Killarney
            Location(315000.0, 230000.0),   // Wicklow / Dublin Mountains
            Location(125000.0, 280000.0),   // Galway / Connemara
            Location(210000.0, 380000.0),   // Sligo / Donegal
            Location(140000.0, 120000.0)    // Cork / Comeragh
        )

        for (loc in testLocations) {
            val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(loc)
            val convertedBackLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(lat, lon)

            val errorX = Math.abs(loc.x - convertedBackLoc.x)
            val errorY = Math.abs(loc.y - convertedBackLoc.y)

            println("Location (${loc.x}, ${loc.y}) -> Lat/Lon ($lat, $lon) -> Back (${convertedBackLoc.x}, ${convertedBackLoc.y}) | Error X: %.4fm, Error Y: %.4fm".format(errorX, errorY))

            assertEquals("X coordinate conversion round-trip error must be < 0.10m for (${loc.x}, ${loc.y})", loc.x, convertedBackLoc.x, 0.10)
            assertEquals("Y coordinate conversion round-trip error must be < 0.10m for (${loc.x}, ${loc.y})", loc.y, convertedBackLoc.y, 0.10)
        }
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/util/RouteParserTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import com.example.irishhike.model.Leg
import com.example.irishhike.model.Route
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class RouteParserTest {

    @Test
    fun `parseCsv converts sample Irish grid CSV into waypoints`() {
        val sampleCsv = RouteParser.generateSampleIrishGridCsv()
        val waypoints = RouteParser.parseCsv(sampleCsv)

        assertEquals(5, waypoints.size)
        assertEquals("Start", waypoints[0].name)
        assertEquals("Colt", waypoints[1].name)
        assertEquals("Summit", waypoints[2].name)
        assertEquals("Ridge", waypoints[3].name)
        assertEquals("Finish", waypoints[4].name)

        assertEquals(120.0, waypoints[0].altitude, 1e-3)
        assertEquals("Heading north toward Colt", waypoints[0].legDescription)
    }

    @Test
    fun `parseFromCsvContent creates linked legs in Route`() {
        val sampleCsv = RouteParser.generateSampleIrishGridCsv()
        val route = Route.parseFromCsvContent("Test Irish Route", sampleCsv)

        assertEquals("Test Irish Route", route.name)
        assertEquals(4, route.numLegs)
        assertTrue("Route length must be positive", route.totalLengthMeters > 0.0)

        var currentLeg: Leg? = route.legs.firstOrNull()
        var legCount = 0
        while (currentLeg != null) {
            legCount++
            assertNotNull("Start waypoint must exist", currentLeg.start)
            assertNotNull("Target waypoint must exist", currentLeg.target)
            currentLeg = currentLeg.next
        }

        assertEquals(4, legCount)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/irishhike/util/OsmTileProjectionTest.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class OsmTileProjectionTest {

    @Test
    fun testTileUniquenessInAreaQuery() {
        val minX = 80000.0
        val maxX = 95000.0
        val minY = 80000.0
        val maxY = 95000.0

        for (z in 8..15) {
            val tiles = OsmTileProjectionUtils.getTilesForMetricArea(minX, maxX, minY, maxY, zoom = z)
            val uniqueKeys = tiles.map { "${it.zoom}/${it.tileX}/${it.tileY}" }.toSet()

            assertEquals("All queried tiles at zoom $z must be strictly unique", tiles.size, uniqueKeys.size)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun testZoomSubdivisionHalving() {
        val z = 10
        val tileX = 495
        val tileY = 330

        val parentCorners = OsmTileProjectionUtils.tileToMetricCorners(z, tileX, tileY)

        val child00 = OsmTileProjectionUtils.tileToMetricCorners(z + 1, tileX * 2, tileY * 2)

        val parentSpanX = parentCorners.spanX
        val parentSpanY = parentCorners.spanY

        val childSpanX = child00.spanX
        val childSpanY = child00.spanY

        // Child physical width and height must be approximately 1/2 of parent
        assertEquals(0.5, childSpanX / parentSpanX, 0.05)
        assertEquals(0.5, childSpanY / parentSpanY, 0.05)

        // Confirm child tile connects to parent bounding box
        assertEquals(parentCorners.minX, child00.minX, 500.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun testMetricCornerMonotonicity() {
        val corners = OsmTileProjectionUtils.tileToMetricCorners(10, 495, 330)

        assertTrue("East corner X must be greater than West corner X", corners.neMetric.x > corners.nwMetric.x)
        assertTrue("North corner Y must be greater than South corner Y", corners.nwMetric.y > corners.swMetric.y)
    }

    @Test
    fun testTilePhysicalMetricDimensions() {
        var prevSpanX = Double.MAX_VALUE

        for (z in 8..15) {
            val tx = OsmTileProjectionUtils.lonToTileX(-9.5, z)
            val ty = OsmTileProjectionUtils.latToTileY(52.0, z)
            val corners = OsmTileProjectionUtils.tileToMetricCorners(z, tx, ty)
            val currentSpanX = corners.spanX

            assertTrue("Metric tile span at zoom $z ($currentSpanX) must be smaller than zoom ${z - 1} ($prevSpanX)", currentSpanX < prevSpanX)
            prevSpanX = currentSpanX
        }
    }
}
