class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    private val viewModel: HikeViewModel by viewModels()

    private val permissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
    ) { permissions ->
        val fineLocationGranted = permissions[Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION] ?: false
        if (fineLocationGranted) {
            viewModel.startTracking(this)
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(this, "Location permission required for PDR tracking", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        HikeRepository.initPreferences(this)

        setContent {
            PDRWithEKFTestTheme {
                MainDiagnosticScreen(
                    viewModel = viewModel,
                    onLoadRouteUri = { uri ->
                        viewModel.loadRouteFromUri(this, uri)
                    },
                    onLoadSampleRoute = {
                        viewModel.loadSampleRoute()
                    },
                    onLoadTelemetryTrackUri = { uri ->
                        viewModel.loadTelemetryTrackFromUri(this, uri)
                    },
                    onRequestPermissionsAndStart = {
                        checkAndRequestPermissions()
                    },
                    onStopTracking = {
                        viewModel.stopTracking(this)
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }

    private fun checkAndRequestPermissions() {
        val permissionsToRequest = mutableListOf(
            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION
        )

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            permissionsToRequest.add(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
        }

        val missingPermissions = permissionsToRequest.filter {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, it) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        }

        if (missingPermissions.isEmpty()) {
            viewModel.startTracking(this)
        } else {
            permissionLauncher.launch(missingPermissions.toTypedArray())
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun MainDiagnosticScreen(
    viewModel: HikeViewModel,
    onLoadRouteUri: (Uri) -> Unit,
    onLoadSampleRoute: () -> Unit,
    onLoadTelemetryTrackUri: (Uri) -> Unit,
    onRequestPermissionsAndStart: () -> Unit,
    onStopTracking: () -> Unit
) {
    val ekfState by viewModel.ekfState.collectAsState()
    val isTracking by viewModel.isTracking.collectAsState()
    val breadcrumbs by viewModel.breadcrumbs.collectAsState()
    val activeRoute by viewModel.activeRoute.collectAsState()
    val currentTargetWaypoint by viewModel.currentTargetWaypoint.collectAsState()
    val mapConfig by viewModel.mapConfig.collectAsState()
    val breadcrumbConfig by viewModel.breadcrumbConfig.collectAsState()
    val hikeConfig by viewModel.hikeConfig.collectAsState()
    val activeLeg by viewModel.activeLeg.collectAsState()
    val currentLegIndex by viewModel.currentLegIndex.collectAsState()
    val navigationMessages by viewModel.navigationMessages.collectAsState()
    val relativeBearingCorrection by viewModel.relativeBearingCorrection.collectAsState()

    val isPlaybackPlaying by viewModel.isPlaybackPlaying.collectAsState()
    val isPlaybackPaused by viewModel.isPlaybackPaused.collectAsState()
    val playbackSpeed by viewModel.playbackSpeed.collectAsState()
    val playbackProgress by viewModel.playbackProgress.collectAsState()
    val playbackEvents by viewModel.playbackEvents.collectAsState()

    val context = LocalContext.current
    var showRouteEditor by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(navigationMessages) {
        if (navigationMessages.any { it.text.contains("Hike completed", ignoreCase = true) }) {
            if (breadcrumbs.isNotEmpty()) {
                showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog = true
            }
        }
    }

    val filePickerLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        uri?.let { onLoadRouteUri(it) }
    }

    val trackPickerLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        uri?.let { onLoadTelemetryTrackUri(it) }
    }

    if (showRouteEditor) {
        RouteEditorScreen(
            initialRoute = activeRoute,
            onSaveRoute = { _, newRoute ->
                viewModel.saveEditedRoute(activeRoute?.name ?: "Edited Route", newRoute)
            },
            onBack = {
                showRouteEditor = false
            }
        )
    } else {
        Scaffold(
            topBar = {
                TopAppBar(
                    title = {
                        Column {
                            if (activeRoute == null) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "PDR & 6-State EKF Diagnostic",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                )
                            } else {
                                val route = activeRoute!!
                                val lengthKm = route.totalLengthMeters / 1000.0
                                Text(
                                    text = route.name,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "${route.numLegs} Legs • %.2f km".format(lengthKm),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer.copy(alpha = 0.8f)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    actions = {
                        Row(
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            if (activeRoute == null) {
                                OutlinedButton(
                                    onClick = onLoadSampleRoute,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(end = 2.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Text("Sample", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall)
                                }
                            }
                            Button(
                                onClick = { filePickerLauncher.launch("*/*") },
                                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                    containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
                                )
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    if (activeRoute == null) "Load Route" else "Load New",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
                        containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                        titleContentColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer
                    )
                )
            }
        ) { innerPadding ->
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(innerPadding)
                    .padding(8.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                HikeNavigationPanel(
                    activeRoute = activeRoute,
                    activeLeg = activeLeg,
                    activeEkfState = ekfState,
                    currentLegIndex = currentLegIndex,
                    onPrevLeg = { viewModel.goToPreviousLeg() },
                    onNextLeg = { viewModel.goToNextLeg() },
                    onEditRoute = { showRouteEditor = true }
                )

                if (mapConfig.showTelemetryViewport) {
                    TelemetryDashboard(ekfState = ekfState)
                }

                NavigationMessagePanel(messages = navigationMessages)

                MapScreen(
                    route = activeRoute,
                    breadcrumbs = breadcrumbs,
                    activeEkfState = ekfState,
                    currentTargetWaypoint = currentTargetWaypoint,
                    mapConfig = mapConfig,
                    breadcrumbConfig = breadcrumbConfig,
                    hikeConfig = hikeConfig,
                    activeLeg = activeLeg,
                    relativeBearingCorrection = relativeBearingCorrection,
                    onUpdateMapConfig = { viewModel.updateMapConfig(it) },
                    onUpdateBreadcrumbConfig = { viewModel.updateBreadcrumbConfig(it) },
                    onUpdateHikeConfig = { viewModel.updateHikeConfig(it) },
                    onIntervalSelected = { intervalMs -> viewModel.setGpsPollingInterval(context, intervalMs) },
                    onLoadTelemetryTrack = { trackPickerLauncher.launch("*/*") },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .weight(1f)
                )

                ControlPanel(
                    isTracking = isTracking,
                    onToggleTracking = {
                        if (isTracking) {
                            onStopTracking()
                            if (breadcrumbs.isNotEmpty()) {
                                showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog = true
                            }
                        } else {
                            onRequestPermissionsAndStart()
                        }
                    },
                    onLoadTrack = { trackPickerLauncher.launch("*/*") },
                    hasLoadedTrack = playbackEvents.isNotEmpty(),
                    isPlaybackPlaying = isPlaybackPlaying,
                    isPlaybackPaused = isPlaybackPaused,
                    playbackSpeed = playbackSpeed,
                    playbackCurrentIndex = playbackProgress.currentIndex,
                    playbackTotalEvents = playbackProgress.totalEvents,
                    onPlayPlayback = { viewModel.startPlayback() },
                    onPausePlayback = { viewModel.pausePlayback() },
                    onStopPlayback = { viewModel.stopPlayback() },
                    onStepBackPlayback = { viewModel.stepBackPlayback() },
                    onStepForwardPlayback = { viewModel.stepForwardPlayback() },
                    onSpeedSelected = { speed -> viewModel.setPlaybackSpeed(speed) }
                )
            }
        }

        if (showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog) {
            AlertDialog(
                onDismissRequest = { showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog = false },
                title = { Text("Save Breadcrumbs?", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
                text = { Text("Would you like to save your recorded breadcrumb track as a .gpx file (bc-yyyymmdd-hhmm.gpx)?") },
                confirmButton = {
                    TextButton(
                        onClick = {
                            showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog = false
                            viewModel.exportBreadcrumbs(context)
                        }
                    ) {
                        Text("Save")
                    }
                },
                dismissButton = {
                    TextButton(
                        onClick = { showSaveBreadcrumbsDialog = false }
                    ) {
                        Text("Discard")
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/CoordinateUtils.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.*
import com.example.irishhike.model.Location

// --- Internal Data Classes & Maps ---

private data class LatLon(val latitude: Double, val longitude: Double)

private data class IrishGridReference(
    val letter: Char,
    val easting: Double,
    val northing: Double
) {
    override fun toString(): String {
        val e = easting.toInt().toString().padStart(5, '0')
        val n = northing.toInt().toString().padStart(5, '0')
        return "$letter $e$n"
    }
}

private data class FullCoordinates(val x: Double, val y: Double)

private data class GridOffset(val eastingOffset: Double, val northingOffset: Double)

private val letterCorrectionMap = mapOf(
    'A' to GridOffset(70.07, 146.81), 'B' to GridOffset(86.22, 147.32),
    'C' to GridOffset(106.15, 148.39), 'D' to GridOffset(121.62, 149.54),
    'F' to GridOffset(70.73, 125.83), 'G' to GridOffset(87.12, 126.65),
    'H' to GridOffset(107.14, 127.72), 'J' to GridOffset(122.88, 128.59),
    'L' to GridOffset(71.34, 102.25), 'M' to GridOffset(88.16, 103.47),
    'N' to GridOffset(108.22, 104.51), 'O' to GridOffset(124.39, 105.09),
    'Q' to GridOffset(72.04, 78.80), 'R' to GridOffset(89.28, 80.07),
    'S' to GridOffset(109.23, 81.13), 'T' to GridOffset(125.81, 81.69),
    'V' to GridOffset(72.71, 57.07), 'W' to GridOffset(90.45, 58.27),
    'X' to GridOffset(110.09, 59.33), 'Y' to GridOffset(127.15, 59.99)
)

private val dynamic10kmOffsetMap = mutableMapOf<String, GridOffset>()

private val gridLetters = arrayOf(
    arrayOf('A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E'),
    arrayOf('F', 'G', 'H', 'J', 'K'),
    arrayOf('L', 'M', 'N', 'O', 'P'),
    arrayOf('Q', 'R', 'S', 'T', 'U'),
    arrayOf('V', 'W', 'X', 'Y', 'Z')
)

private val gridLetterMap = mapOf(
    'A' to Pair(0.0, 400000.0), 'B' to Pair(100000.0, 400000.0), 'C' to Pair(200000.0, 400000.0), 'D' to Pair(300000.0, 400000.0), 'E' to Pair(400000.0, 400000.0),
    'F' to Pair(0.0, 300000.0), 'G' to Pair(100000.0, 300000.0), 'H' to Pair(200000.0, 300000.0), 'J' to Pair(300000.0, 300000.0), 'K' to Pair(400000.0, 300000.0),
    'L' to Pair(0.0, 200000.0), 'M' to Pair(100000.0, 200000.0), 'N' to Pair(200000.0, 200000.0), 'O' to Pair(300000.0, 200000.0), 'P' to Pair(400000.0, 200000.0),
    'Q' to Pair(0.0, 100000.0), 'R' to Pair(100000.0, 100000.0), 'S' to Pair(200000.0, 100000.0), 'T' to Pair(300000.0, 100000.0), 'U' to Pair(400000.0, 100000.0),
    'V' to Pair(0.0, 0.0),      'W' to Pair(100000.0, 0.0),      'X' to Pair(200000.0, 0.0),      'Y' to Pair(300000.0, 0.0),      'Z' to Pair(400000.0, 0.0)
)

private fun loadIrishGridOffsetsHelper(context: Context? = null, fileName: String = "irish_grid_offsets_10km.json") {
        try {
            val jsonString: String? = if (context != null) {
                try {
                    context.assets.open(fileName).bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    null
                }
            } else null

            val finalJson = jsonString ?: run {
                val stream = CoordinateUtils::class.java.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream("assets/$fileName")
                    ?: CoordinateUtils::class.java.classLoader?.getResourceAsStream(fileName)
                    ?: java.io.File("app/src/main/assets/$fileName").takeIf { it.exists() }?.inputStream()
                    ?: java.io.File("src/main/assets/$fileName").takeIf { it.exists() }?.inputStream()
                stream?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() }
            }

            if (finalJson != null) {
                val jsonObject = JSONObject(finalJson)
                dynamic10kmOffsetMap.clear()
                for (key in jsonObject.keys()) {
                    val array = jsonObject.getJSONArray(key)
                    dynamic10kmOffsetMap[key] = GridOffset(array.getDouble(0), array.getDouble(1))
                }
                Log.d("CoordinateUtils", "Loaded ${dynamic10kmOffsetMap.size} 10km grid offsets.")
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("CoordinateUtils", "Failed to load 10km offsets: ${e.message}. Using 100km fallbacks.")
        }
    }

// --- Internal Irish Grid Converter ---

private object IrishGridConverter {
    private const val A = 6377340.189
    private const val B = 6356034.446
    private const val F0 = 1.000035

    private const val LAT0 = 53.5
    private const val LON0 = -8.0
    private const val N0 = 250000.0
    private const val E0 = 200000.0

    private const val WGS84_A = 6378137.0
    private const val WGS84_B = 6356752.3142

    private val gridLetters = arrayOf(
        arrayOf('A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E'),
        arrayOf('F', 'G', 'H', 'J', 'K'),
        arrayOf('L', 'M', 'N', 'O', 'P'),
        arrayOf('Q', 'R', 'S', 'T', 'U'),
        arrayOf('V', 'W', 'X', 'Y', 'Z')
    )

    fun Wgs84ToIrishGrid(latLon: LatLon): IrishGridReference {
        val irl75 = wgs84ToIrl75Internal(latLon)
        val rawRef = projectToGridInternal(irl75)

        val correction = getBestOffsetInternal(rawRef.letter, rawRef.easting, rawRef.northing)
        return IrishGridReference(
            letter = rawRef.letter,
            easting = rawRef.easting + correction.eastingOffset,
            northing = rawRef.northing + correction.northingOffset
        )
    }

    fun IrishGridToWgs84(gridRefStr: String): LatLon {
        val clean = gridRefStr.replace(Regex("[^A-Z0-9]"), "").uppercase()
        require(clean.length >= 2) { "Invalid grid reference: $gridRefStr" }
        val letter = clean[0]

        val rawXY = parseGridReferenceToXYInternal(clean)
        val roughLocalE = rawXY.x - getGridLetterEastingOffsetInternal(letter)
        val roughLocalN = rawXY.y % 100000.0

        val correction = getBestOffsetInternal(letter, roughLocalE, roughLocalN)
        val unadjustedX = rawXY.x - correction.eastingOffset
        val unadjustedY = rawXY.y - correction.northingOffset

        val irl75 = inverseProjectToLatLonInternal(unadjustedX, unadjustedY)
        return irl75ToWgs84Internal(irl75)
    }

    fun getBestOffsetPublic(letter: Char, localEasting: Double, localNorthing: Double): GridOffset {
        return getBestOffsetInternal(letter, localEasting, localNorthing)
    }

    fun metricXYToWgs84(unadjustedX: Double, unadjustedY: Double): LatLon {
        val irl75 = inverseProjectToLatLonInternal(unadjustedX, unadjustedY)
        return irl75ToWgs84Internal(irl75)
    }

    private fun getBestOffsetInternal(letter: Char, localEasting: Double, localNorthing: Double): GridOffset {
        val eastBlock = (localEasting / 10000).toInt().coerceIn(0, 9)
        val northBlock = (localNorthing / 10000).toInt().coerceIn(0, 9)
        val cellKey = "$letter-$eastBlock-$northBlock"

        return dynamic10kmOffsetMap[cellKey] ?: letterCorrectionMap[letter] ?: GridOffset(0.0, 0.0)
    }

    private fun getGridLetterEastingOffsetInternal(letter: Char): Double {
        val columns = mapOf(
            'A' to 0, 'F' to 0, 'L' to 0, 'Q' to 0, 'V' to 0,
            'B' to 1, 'G' to 1, 'M' to 1, 'R' to 1, 'W' to 1,
            'C' to 2, 'H' to 2, 'N' to 2, 'S' to 2, 'X' to 2,
            'D' to 3, 'J' to 3, 'O' to 3, 'T' to 3, 'Y' to 3,
            'E' to 4, 'K' to 4, 'P' to 4, 'U' to 4, 'Z' to 4
        )
        return ((columns[letter.uppercaseChar()] ?: 0) * 100000).toDouble()
    }

    private fun parseGridReferenceToXYInternal(cleanRef: String): FullCoordinates {
        val letter = cleanRef[0]
        val numericPart = cleanRef.substring(1)
        val halfLen = numericPart.length / 2

        val localE = numericPart.substring(0, halfLen).padEnd(5, '0').take(5).toDouble()
        val localN = numericPart.substring(halfLen).padEnd(5, '0').take(5).toDouble()

        var baseE = 0.0
        var baseN = 0.0
        outer@ for (r in gridLetters.indices) {
            for (c in gridLetters[r].indices) {
                if (gridLetters[r][c] == letter) {
                    baseE = c * 100000.0
                    baseN = (4 - r) * 100000.0
                    break@outer
                }
            }
        }
        return FullCoordinates(baseE + localE, baseN + localN)
    }

    private fun wgs84ToIrl75Internal(latLon: LatLon): LatLon {
        val lat1 = Math.toRadians(latLon.latitude)
        val lon1 = Math.toRadians(latLon.longitude)
        val eSq1 = (WGS84_A * WGS84_A - WGS84_B * WGS84_B) / (WGS84_A * WGS84_A)
        val nu1 = WGS84_A / sqrt(1.0 - eSq1 * sin(lat1) * sin(lat1))
        val x1 = nu1 * cos(lat1) * cos(lon1)
        val y1 = nu1 * cos(lat1) * sin(lon1)
        val z1 = nu1 * (1.0 - eSq1) * sin(lat1)

        val dx = -482.530
        val dy = 130.596
        val dz = -564.557
        val s = -8.15e-6
        val rx = Math.toRadians(1.042 / 3600.0)
        val ry = Math.toRadians(0.214 / 3600.0)
        val rz = Math.toRadians(-0.631 / 3600.0)

        val scale = 1.0 - s
        val x0 = x1 - dx
        val y0 = y1 - dy
        val z0 = z1 - dz

        val x2 = scale * (x0 + rz * y0 - ry * z0)
        val y2 = scale * (y0 - rz * x0 + rx * z0)
        val z2 = scale * (z0 + ry * x0 - rx * y0)

        val eSq2 = (A * A - B * B) / (A * A)
        val p = sqrt(x2 * x2 + y2 * y2)
        var lat2 = atan2(z2, p * (1.0 - eSq2))
        var lon2 = atan2(y2, x2)
        for (i in 0..4) {
            val nu2 = A / sqrt(1.0 - eSq2 * sin(lat2) * sin(lat2))
            lat2 = atan2(z2 + eSq2 * nu2 * sin(lat2), p)
        }
        return LatLon(Math.toDegrees(lat2), Math.toDegrees(lon2))
    }

    private fun irl75ToWgs84Internal(latLon: LatLon): LatLon {
        val lat1 = Math.toRadians(latLon.latitude)
        val lon1 = Math.toRadians(latLon.longitude)
        val eSq1 = (A * A - B * B) / (A * A)
        val nu1 = A / sqrt(1.0 - eSq1 * sin(lat1) * sin(lat1))
        val x1 = nu1 * cos(lat1) * cos(lon1)
        val y1 = nu1 * cos(lat1) * sin(lon1)
        val z1 = nu1 * (1.0 - eSq1) * sin(lat1)

        val dx = -482.530
        val dy = 130.596
        val dz = -564.557
        val s = -8.15e-6
        val rx = Math.toRadians(1.042 / 3600.0)
        val ry = Math.toRadians(0.214 / 3600.0)
        val rz = Math.toRadians(-0.631 / 3600.0)

        val scale = 1.0 + s
        val x2 = dx + scale * x1 - rz * y1 + ry * z1
        val y2 = dy + scale * y1 + rz * x1 - rx * z1
        val z2 = dz + scale * z1 - ry * x1 + rx * y1

        val eSq2 = (WGS84_A * WGS84_A - WGS84_B * WGS84_B) / (WGS84_A * WGS84_A)
        val p = sqrt(x2 * x2 + y2 * y2)
        var lat2 = atan2(z2, p * (1.0 - eSq2))
        var lon2 = atan2(y2, x2)
        for (i in 0..4) {
            val nu2 = WGS84_A / sqrt(1.0 - eSq2 * sin(lat2) * sin(lat2))
            lat2 = atan2(z2 + eSq2 * nu2 * sin(lat2), p)
        }
        return LatLon(Math.toDegrees(lat2), Math.toDegrees(lon2))
    }

    private fun computeMeridionalArc(lat: Double, lat0: Double, b: Double, n: Double): Double {
        val n2 = n * n
        val n3 = n * n * n
        val ma = (1.0 + n + (5.0 / 4.0) * n2 + (5.0 / 4.0) * n3) * (lat - lat0)
        val mb = (3.0 * n + 3.0 * n2 + (21.0 / 8.0) * n3) * sin(lat - lat0) * cos(lat + lat0)
        val mc = ((15.0 / 8.0) * n2 + (15.0 / 8.0) * n3) * sin(2.0 * (lat - lat0)) * cos(2.0 * (lat + lat0))
        val md = (35.0 / 24.0) * n3 * sin(3.0 * (lat - lat0)) * cos(3.0 * (lat + lat0))
        return b * (ma - mb + mc - md)
    }

    private fun projectToGridInternal(latLon: LatLon): IrishGridReference {
        val latRad = Math.toRadians(latLon.latitude)
        val lonRad = Math.toRadians(latLon.longitude)
        val phi0 = Math.toRadians(LAT0)
        val lambda0 = Math.toRadians(LON0)

        val e2 = (A * A - B * B) / (A * A)
        val n = (A - B) / (A + B)
        val sinLat = sin(latRad)
        val cosLat = cos(latRad)
        val tanLat = tan(latRad)

        val nu = A * F0 / sqrt(1.0 - e2 * sinLat * sinLat)
        val rho = A * F0 * (1.0 - e2) / (1.0 - e2 * sinLat * sinLat).pow(1.5)
        val m = computeMeridionalArc(latRad, phi0, B, n)
        val dLambda = lonRad - lambda0

        val term1 = nu * cosLat * dLambda
        val term2 = nu * cosLat.pow(3) * (nu / rho - tanLat * tanLat) * dLambda.pow(3) / 6.0
        val term3 = nu * cosLat.pow(5) * (5.0 - 18.0 * tanLat * tanLat + tanLat.pow(4)) * dLambda.pow(5) / 120.0
        val eastingTotal = E0 + F0 * (term1 + term2 + term3)

        val nTerm1 = m
        val nTerm2 = nu * sinLat * cosLat * dLambda.pow(2) / 2.0
        val nTerm3 = nu * sinLat * cosLat.pow(3) * (5.0 - tanLat * tanLat + 9.0 * (nu / rho - 1.0)) * dLambda.pow(4) / 24.0
        val northingTotal = N0 + F0 * (nTerm1 + nTerm2 + nTerm3)

        val col = (eastingTotal / 100000.0).toInt().coerceIn(0, 4)
        val row = (4 - (northingTotal / 100000.0).toInt()).coerceIn(0, 4)

        return IrishGridReference(gridLetters[row][col], eastingTotal % 100000.0, northingTotal % 100000.0)
    }

    private fun inverseProjectToLatLonInternal(x: Double, y: Double): LatLon {
        val phi0 = Math.toRadians(LAT0)
        val lambda0 = Math.toRadians(LON0)
        val e2 = (A * A - B * B) / (A * A)
        val n = (A - B) / (A + B)

        var phiPrime = phi0 + (y - N0) / (A * F0)
        var m: Double
        do {
            m = computeMeridionalArc(phiPrime, phi0, B, n)
            phiPrime = (y - N0 - (m * F0)) / (A * F0) + phiPrime
        } while (abs(y - N0 - (m * F0)) >= 0.00001)

        val sinPhi = sin(phiPrime)
        val cosPhi = cos(phiPrime)
        val tanPhi = tan(phiPrime)
        val nuPrime = A * F0 / sqrt(1.0 - e2 * sinPhi * sinPhi)
        val rhoPrime = A * F0 * (1.0 - e2) / (1.0 - e2 * sinPhi * sinPhi).pow(1.5)
        val etaSqPrime = nuPrime / rhoPrime - 1.0

        val xRel = x - E0
        val secPhi = 1.0 / cosPhi
        val vii = tanPhi / (2.0 * rhoPrime * nuPrime * F0 * F0)
        val viii = tanPhi / (24.0 * rhoPrime * nuPrime.pow(3) * F0.pow(4)) * (5.0 + 3.0 * tanPhi * tanPhi + etaSqPrime)

        val lat = phiPrime - vii * xRel.pow(2) + viii * xRel.pow(4)
        val xXi = secPhi / (nuPrime * F0)
        val xXii = secPhi / (6.0 * nuPrime.pow(3) * F0.pow(3)) * (nuPrime / rhoPrime + 2.0 * tanPhi * tanPhi)

        val lon = lambda0 + xXi * xRel - xXii * xRel.pow(3)

        return LatLon(Math.toDegrees(lat), Math.toDegrees(lon))
    }
}

// --- Public CoordinateUtils Object exposing 4 Location-based methods ---

object CoordinateUtils {

    fun loadIrishGridOffsets(context: Context? = null) {
        loadIrishGridOffsetsHelper(context)
    }

    /**
     * Converts WGS84 latitude and longitude into Irish National Grid metric Location (x, y, altitude).
     * Uses 100km subgrid local Easting and Northing (0.0 to 99999.9m) for Alphanumeric Irish Grid compatibility.
     */
    fun latLonToMetric(lat: Double, lon: Double): Location {
        val gridRef = IrishGridConverter.Wgs84ToIrishGrid(LatLon(lat, lon))
        val baseOffset = gridLetterMap[gridRef.letter] ?: Pair(0.0, 0.0)
        return Location(
            x = baseOffset.first + gridRef.easting,
            y = baseOffset.second + gridRef.northing,
            altitude = 0.0
        )
    }

    /**
     * Converts Irish National Grid metric Location (x, y) into WGS84 latitude and longitude Pair(lat, lon).
     */
    fun metricToLatLon(location: Location): Pair<Double, Double> {
        val col = (location.x / 100000.0).toInt().coerceIn(0, 4)
        val row = (4 - (location.y / 100000.0).toInt()).coerceIn(0, 4)
        val letter = gridLetters[row][col]

        val localE = (location.x % 100000.0).let { if (it < 0) it + 100000.0 else it }
        val localN = (location.y % 100000.0).let { if (it < 0) it + 100000.0 else it }

        val letterDefault = letterCorrectionMap[letter] ?: GridOffset(0.0, 0.0)
        val roughUnadjustedE = (localE - letterDefault.eastingOffset).coerceIn(0.0, 99999.0)
        val roughUnadjustedN = (localN - letterDefault.northingOffset).coerceIn(0.0, 99999.0)

        val correction = IrishGridConverter.getBestOffsetPublic(letter, roughUnadjustedE, roughUnadjustedN)
        val unadjustedX = location.x - correction.eastingOffset
        val unadjustedY = location.y - correction.northingOffset

        val latLon = IrishGridConverter.metricXYToWgs84(unadjustedX, unadjustedY)
        return Pair(latLon.latitude, latLon.longitude)
    }

    /**
     * Computes metric Euclidean distance in meters between two Location objects.
     */
    fun getDistance(loc1: Location, loc2: Location): Double {
        return hypot(loc2.x - loc1.x, loc2.y - loc1.y)
    }

    /**
     * Computes grid bearing in degrees (0.0 to 360.0, clockwise from Grid North) from loc1 to loc2.
     */
    fun getBearing(loc1: Location, loc2: Location): Double {
        val dx = loc2.x - loc1.x
        val dy = loc2.y - loc1.y
        var angle = Math.toDegrees(atan2(dx, dy))
        if (angle < 0) {
            angle += 360.0
        }
        return angle
    }

    // --- Private Internal Utility Helpers ---

    private fun parseGridReferenceInternal(ref: String): Location {
        val cleaned = ref.trim().uppercase()
        if (cleaned.isEmpty()) return Location(0.0, 0.0)

        val hasLetter = cleaned[0] in 'A'..'Z' && cleaned[0] != 'I'
        val letter = if (hasLetter) cleaned[0] else 'V'
        val numericPart = if (hasLetter) cleaned.substring(1).replace(Regex("\\s+"), "") else cleaned.replace(Regex("\\s+"), "")

        val halfLen = numericPart.length / 2
        val eastingStr = if (halfLen > 0) numericPart.substring(0, halfLen) else "0"
        val northingStr = if (halfLen > 0) numericPart.substring(halfLen) else numericPart

        val padFactor = 5 - eastingStr.length
        val multiplier = 10.0.pow(padFactor.coerceAtLeast(0))

        val localE = (eastingStr.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0) * multiplier
        val localN = (northingStr.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0) * multiplier

        val baseOffset = gridLetterMap[letter] ?: Pair(0.0, 0.0)
        val globalE = baseOffset.first + localE
        val globalN = baseOffset.second + localN

        return Location(globalE, globalN)
    }

    internal fun parseGridRefToLocation(ref: String, altitude: Double = 0.0): Location {
        val loc = parseGridReferenceInternal(ref)
        return Location(loc.x, loc.y, altitude)
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/TtsManager.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import android.content.Context
import android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech
import android.speech.tts.UtteranceProgressListener
import android.util.Log
import java.util.Locale

class TtsManager(
    context: Context,
    private val onSpeechDone: (() -> Unit)? = null
) : TextToSpeech.OnInitListener {

    private var tts: TextToSpeech? = TextToSpeech(context.applicationContext, this)
    private var isInitialized = false
    private var isSpeaking = false

    init {
        tts?.setOnUtteranceProgressListener(object : UtteranceProgressListener() {
            override fun onStart(utteranceId: String?) {
                isSpeaking = true
                Log.d("TtsManager", "TTS Started: $utteranceId")
            }

            override fun onDone(utteranceId: String?) {
                isSpeaking = false
                Log.d("TtsManager", "TTS Done: $utteranceId")
                onSpeechDone?.invoke()
            }

            @Deprecated("Deprecated in Java")
            override fun onError(utteranceId: String?) {
                isSpeaking = false
                Log.e("TtsManager", "TTS Error: $utteranceId")
                onSpeechDone?.invoke()
            }

            override fun onError(utteranceId: String?, errorCode: Int) {
                isSpeaking = false
                Log.e("TtsManager", "TTS Error ($errorCode): $utteranceId")
                onSpeechDone?.invoke()
            }
        })
    }

    override fun onInit(status: Int) {
        if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
            val result = tts?.setLanguage(Locale.UK)
            if (result == TextToSpeech.LANG_MISSING_DATA || result == TextToSpeech.LANG_NOT_SUPPORTED) {
                tts?.setLanguage(Locale.US)
            }
            isInitialized = true
            Log.i("TtsManager", "TextToSpeech initialized successfully.")
            onSpeechDone?.invoke()
        } else {
            Log.e("TtsManager", "TextToSpeech initialization failed with status: $status")
        }
    }

    fun isReady(): Boolean = isInitialized

    fun speak(text: String, queueMode: Int = TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH) {
        if (!isInitialized || text.isBlank()) return

        val utteranceId = "HikeMsg_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
        tts?.speak(text, queueMode, null, utteranceId)
    }

    fun stop() {
        if (isInitialized) {
            tts?.stop()
            isSpeaking = false
        }
    }

    fun shutdown() {
        if (isInitialized) {
            tts?.stop()
            tts?.shutdown()
            isInitialized = false
            isSpeaking = false
        }
    }
}

    fun logError(message: String) {
        debugLogs.add(message)
    }

    fun clearLogs() {
        debugLogs.clear()
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/OsmTileManager.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.util.Log
import android.util.LruCache
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.ImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import com.example.irishhike.model.Route
import kotlinx.coroutines.DelicateCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.GlobalScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

object OsmTileManager {

    private val memoryCache = LruCache<String, ImageBitmap>(1000)
    private val cornerCache = LruCache<String, MetricTileCorners>(1000)
    private val loadingKeys = java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet<String>()

    // Reusable single static fallback bitmap to avoid allocation thrashing/GC ANR during tile loading
    private val fallbackImageBitmap: ImageBitmap by lazy {
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(256, 256, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(bitmap)

        val bgPaint = Paint().apply {
            color = android.graphics.Color.parseColor("#151D2A")
            style = Paint.Style.FILL
        }
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, 256f, 256f, bgPaint)

        val gridPaint = Paint().apply {
            color = android.graphics.Color.parseColor("#233146")
            strokeWidth = 1f
            style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        }
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, 256f, 256f, gridPaint)

        val textPaint = Paint().apply {
            color = android.graphics.Color.parseColor("#3B4D66")
            textSize = 18f
            isAntiAlias = true
        }
        canvas.drawText("OSM Map Tile", 12f, 28f, textPaint)

        bitmap.asImageBitmap()
    }

    // Ireland WGS84 Bounding Box
    const val IRELAND_MIN_LAT = 51.4
    const val IRELAND_MAX_LAT = 55.4
    const val IRELAND_MIN_LON = -10.5
    const val IRELAND_MAX_LON = -5.5

    // Reactive Compose state trigger to automatically re-render map canvas when background tiles finish decoding from disk
    val tileCacheVersion = androidx.compose.runtime.mutableIntStateOf(0)

    fun clearCache() {
        memoryCache.evictAll()
        cornerCache.evictAll()
        tileCacheVersion.intValue++
    }

    fun lonToTileX(lon: Double, zoom: Int): Int = OsmTileProjectionUtils.lonToTileX(lon, zoom)

    fun latToTileY(lat: Double, zoom: Int): Int = OsmTileProjectionUtils.latToTileY(lat, zoom)

    fun getTileCorners(zoom: Int, tileX: Int, tileY: Int): MetricTileCorners {
        val cacheKey = "$zoom/$tileX/$tileY"
        cornerCache.get(cacheKey)?.let { return it }

        val corners = OsmTileProjectionUtils.tileToMetricCorners(zoom, tileX, tileY)
        cornerCache.put(cacheKey, corners)
        return corners
    }

    fun hasTileOnDiskOrCache(context: Context, zoom: Int, tileX: Int, tileY: Int): Boolean {
        val cacheKey = "$zoom/$tileX/$tileY"
        if (memoryCache.get(cacheKey) != null) return true
        val tileFile = File(context.filesDir, "tiles/$zoom/$tileX/$tileY.png")
        return tileFile.exists() && tileFile.length() > 0L
    }

    @OptIn(DelicateCoroutinesApi::class)
    fun getTileBitmapFromCacheOnly(context: Context, zoom: Int, tileX: Int, tileY: Int): ImageBitmap? {
        val cacheKey = "$zoom/$tileX/$tileY"
        memoryCache.get(cacheKey)?.let { return it }

        if (!loadingKeys.add(cacheKey)) return null

        GlobalScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                var pZoom = zoom
                var pX = tileX
                var pY = tileY

                while (pZoom >= 8) {
                    val pFile = File(context.filesDir, "tiles/$pZoom/$pX/$pY.png")
                    if (pFile.exists() && pFile.length() > 0) {
                        val parentBitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(pFile.absolutePath)
                        if (parentBitmap != null) {
                            val deltaZ = zoom - pZoom
                            if (deltaZ == 0) {
                                memoryCache.put(cacheKey, parentBitmap.asImageBitmap())
                            } else {
                                val subGridSize = 1 shl deltaZ
                                val subSize = (256 / subGridSize).coerceAtLeast(1)

                                val subX = tileX % subGridSize
                                val subY = tileY % subGridSize

                                val cropX = (subX * subSize).coerceIn(0, 256 - subSize)
                                val cropY = (subY * subSize).coerceIn(0, 256 - subSize)

                                val croppedBitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(parentBitmap, cropX, cropY, subSize, subSize)
                                val scaledBitmap = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(croppedBitmap, 256, 256, true)
                                memoryCache.put(cacheKey, scaledBitmap.asImageBitmap())
                            }
                            notifyTileCacheUpdated()
                            return@launch
                        }
                    }
                    pZoom--
                    pX /= 2
                    pY /= 2
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            } finally {
                loadingKeys.remove(cacheKey)
            }
        }

        return null
    }

    private var pendingNotifyJob: kotlinx.coroutines.Job? = null

    @OptIn(DelicateCoroutinesApi::class)
    @Synchronized
    private fun notifyTileCacheUpdated() {
        if (pendingNotifyJob?.isActive == true) return
        pendingNotifyJob = GlobalScope.launch(Dispatchers.Main) {
            delay(100L)
            tileCacheVersion.intValue++
        }
    }

    fun getTilesForArea(
        minX: Double,
        maxX: Double,
        minY: Double,
        maxY: Double,
        zoom: Int = 10
    ): List<MetricTileCorners> {
        return OsmTileProjectionUtils.getTilesForMetricArea(minX, maxX, minY, maxY, zoom)
    }

    @OptIn(DelicateCoroutinesApi::class)
    fun getTileBitmap(context: Context, zoom: Int, tileX: Int, tileY: Int): ImageBitmap? {
        val cacheKey = "$zoom/$tileX/$tileY"
        memoryCache.get(cacheKey)?.let { return it }

        getTileBitmapFromCacheOnly(context, zoom, tileX, tileY)
        return null
    }

    /**
     * Programmatically downloads OSM tiles for Ireland across specified zoom levels and saves to app files directory.
     */
    suspend fun downloadIrelandTiles(
        context: Context,
        zoomLevels: List<Int> = listOf(8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14),
        onProgress: (Int, Int) -> Unit = { _, _ -> }
    ): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val totalTilesList = mutableListOf<Triple<Int, Int, Int>>()

        for (z in zoomLevels) {
            val minX = lonToTileX(IRELAND_MIN_LON, z)
            val maxX = lonToTileX(IRELAND_MAX_LON, z)
            val minY = latToTileY(IRELAND_MAX_LAT, z)
            val maxY = latToTileY(IRELAND_MIN_LAT, z)

            for (x in minX..maxX) {
                for (y in minY..maxY) {
                    totalTilesList.add(Triple(z, x, y))
                }
            }
        }

        val total = totalTilesList.size
        var downloaded = 0
        var successCount = 0

        Log.i("OsmTileManager", "Starting download of $total tiles for Ireland across zooms $zoomLevels")

        for (tile in totalTilesList) {
            val (z, x, y) = tile
            val tileDir = File(context.filesDir, "tiles/$z/$x")
            if (!tileDir.exists()) tileDir.mkdirs()

            val tileFile = File(tileDir, "$y.png")
            if (!tileFile.exists() || tileFile.length() == 0L) {
                try {
                    val tileUrl = "https://tile.openstreetmap.org/$z/$x/$y.png"
                    val conn = URL(tileUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
                    conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "HikingTool/1.0 (Android PDR Diagnostic App)")
                    conn.connectTimeout = 8000
                    conn.readTimeout = 8000

                    if (conn.responseCode == 200) {
                        conn.inputStream.use { input ->
                            FileOutputStream(tileFile).use { output ->
                                input.copyTo(output)
                            }
                        }
                        if (tileFile.length() > 0) successCount++
                    } else {
                        Log.w("OsmTileManager", "HTTP ${conn.responseCode} for tile $z/$x/$y")
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e("OsmTileManager", "Failed tile $z/$x/$y: ${e.message}")
                }
            } else {
                successCount++
            }
            downloaded++
            onProgress(downloaded, total)
        }

        clearCache()
        TileDownloadManifest.markIrelandDownloaded(context)
        Log.i("OsmTileManager", "Completed tile downloads. Successfully ready: $successCount / $total")
        return@withContext successCount
    }

    /**
     * Programmatically downloads OSM tiles for a specific Route area (with 1km buffer)
     * across specified zoom levels and saves to local file storage.
     */
    suspend fun downloadRouteAreaTiles(
        context: Context,
        route: Route,
        zoomLevels: List<Int> = listOf(10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18),
        onProgress: (Int, Int) -> Unit = { _, _ -> }
    ): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        if (route.waypoints.isEmpty()) return@withContext 0

        var minX = Double.MAX_VALUE
        var maxX = -Double.MAX_VALUE
        var minY = Double.MAX_VALUE
        var maxY = -Double.MAX_VALUE

        route.waypoints.forEach { wp ->
            if (wp.x < minX) minX = wp.x
            if (wp.x > maxX) maxX = wp.x
            if (wp.y < minY) minY = wp.y
            if (wp.y > maxY) maxY = wp.y
        }

        // Add 1km safety buffer around route
        minX -= 1000.0
        maxX += 1000.0
        minY -= 1000.0
        maxY += 1000.0

        val swLatLon = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(Location(minX, minY))
        val neLatLon = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(Location(maxX, maxY))

        val minLat = minOf(swLatLon.first, neLatLon.first)
        val maxLat = maxOf(swLatLon.first, neLatLon.first)
        val minLon = minOf(swLatLon.second, neLatLon.second)
        val maxLon = maxOf(swLatLon.second, neLatLon.second)

        val totalTilesList = mutableListOf<Triple<Int, Int, Int>>()

        for (z in zoomLevels) {
            val minTileX = lonToTileX(minLon, z)
            val maxTileX = lonToTileX(maxLon, z)
            val minTileY = latToTileY(maxLat, z)
            val maxTileY = latToTileY(minLat, z)

            for (x in minTileX..maxTileX) {
                for (y in minTileY..maxTileY) {
                    totalTilesList.add(Triple(z, x, y))
                }
            }
        }

        val total = totalTilesList.size
        var downloaded = 0
        var successCount = 0

        Log.i("OsmTileManager", "Downloading $total route area tiles for '${route.name}' across zooms $zoomLevels")

        for (tile in totalTilesList) {
            val (z, x, y) = tile
            val tileDir = File(context.filesDir, "tiles/$z/$x")
            if (!tileDir.exists()) tileDir.mkdirs()

            val tileFile = File(tileDir, "$y.png")
            if (!tileFile.exists() || tileFile.length() == 0L) {
                try {
                    val tileUrl = "https://tile.openstreetmap.org/$z/$x/$y.png"
                    val conn = URL(tileUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
                    conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "HikingTool/1.0 (Android PDR Diagnostic App)")
                    conn.connectTimeout = 8000
                    conn.readTimeout = 8000

                    if (conn.responseCode == 200) {
                        conn.inputStream.use { input ->
                            FileOutputStream(tileFile).use { output ->
                                input.copyTo(output)
                            }
                        }
                        if (tileFile.length() > 0) successCount++
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e("OsmTileManager", "Failed route tile $z/$x/$y: ${e.message}")
                }
            } else {
                successCount++
            }
            downloaded++
            onProgress(downloaded, total)
        }

        clearCache()
        TileDownloadManifest.markRouteDownloaded(context, route.name)
        Log.i("OsmTileManager", "Completed route area tile downloads: $successCount / $total ready.")
        return@withContext successCount
    }
}

object TileDownloadManifest {
    private val downloadedRoutes = mutableSetOf<String>()
    private var irelandDownloaded = false
    private var isLoaded = false

    fun isRouteDownloaded(context: Context, routeName: String): Boolean {
        ensureLoaded(context)
        return downloadedRoutes.contains(routeName)
    }

    fun isIrelandDownloaded(context: Context): Boolean {
        ensureLoaded(context)
        return irelandDownloaded
    }

    fun markRouteDownloaded(context: Context, routeName: String) {
        ensureLoaded(context)
        downloadedRoutes.add(routeName)
        save(context)
    }

    fun markIrelandDownloaded(context: Context) {
        ensureLoaded(context)
        irelandDownloaded = true
        save(context)
    }

    private fun ensureLoaded(context: Context) {
        if (isLoaded) return
        try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("osm_tile_manifest", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            irelandDownloaded = prefs.getBoolean("ireland_downloaded", false)
            val set = prefs.getStringSet("downloaded_routes", emptySet()) ?: emptySet()
            downloadedRoutes.clear()
            downloadedRoutes.addAll(set)
            isLoaded = true
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun save(context: Context) {
        try {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("osm_tile_manifest", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            prefs.edit()
                .putBoolean("ireland_downloaded", irelandDownloaded)
                .putStringSet("downloaded_routes", downloadedRoutes.toSet())
                .apply()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/OsmTileProjectionUtils.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import com.example.irishhike.model.Location
import kotlin.math.PI
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.atan
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.floor
import kotlin.math.ln
import kotlin.math.pow
import kotlin.math.sinh
import kotlin.math.tan

data class Wgs84Bounds(
    val latNorth: Double,
    val latSouth: Double,
    val lonWest: Double,
    val lonEast: Double
)

data class MetricTileCorners(
    val zoom: Int,
    val tileX: Int,
    val tileY: Int,
    val nwMetric: Location,
    val neMetric: Location,
    val seMetric: Location,
    val swMetric: Location
) {
    val minX: Double get() = minOf(nwMetric.x, neMetric.x, seMetric.x, swMetric.x)
    val maxX: Double get() = maxOf(nwMetric.x, neMetric.x, seMetric.x, swMetric.x)
    val minY: Double get() = minOf(nwMetric.y, neMetric.y, seMetric.y, swMetric.y)
    val maxY: Double get() = maxOf(nwMetric.y, neMetric.y, seMetric.y, swMetric.y)

    val spanX: Double get() = abs(maxX - minX)
    val spanY: Double get() = abs(maxY - minY)
}

object OsmTileProjectionUtils {

    fun lonToTileX(lon: Double, zoom: Int): Int {
        val n = 2.0.pow(zoom)
        return floor((lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * n).toInt().coerceAtLeast(0)
    }

    fun latToTileY(lat: Double, zoom: Int): Int {
        val latRad = Math.toRadians(lat)
        val n = 2.0.pow(zoom)
        return floor((1.0 - ln(tan(latRad) + 1.0 / cos(latRad)) / PI) / 2.0 * n).toInt().coerceAtLeast(0)
    }

    fun tileToWgs84Bounds(zoom: Int, tileX: Int, tileY: Int): Wgs84Bounds {
        val n = 2.0.pow(zoom)
        val lonWest = tileX.toDouble() / n * 360.0 - 180.0
        val lonEast = (tileX + 1).toDouble() / n * 360.0 - 180.0

        val latNorthRad = atan(sinh(PI * (1.0 - 2.0 * tileY / n)))
        val latSouthRad = atan(sinh(PI * (1.0 - 2.0 * (tileY + 1) / n)))

        return Wgs84Bounds(
            latNorth = Math.toDegrees(latNorthRad),
            latSouth = Math.toDegrees(latSouthRad),
            lonWest = lonWest,
            lonEast = lonEast
        )
    }

    fun tileToMetricCorners(zoom: Int, tileX: Int, tileY: Int): MetricTileCorners {
        val wgs84 = tileToWgs84Bounds(zoom, tileX, tileY)

        val nwMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(wgs84.latNorth, wgs84.lonWest)
        val neMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(wgs84.latNorth, wgs84.lonEast)
        val seMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(wgs84.latSouth, wgs84.lonEast)
        val swMetric = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(wgs84.latSouth, wgs84.lonWest)

        return MetricTileCorners(
            zoom = zoom,
            tileX = tileX,
            tileY = tileY,
            nwMetric = nwMetric,
            neMetric = neMetric,
            seMetric = seMetric,
            swMetric = swMetric
        )
    }

    fun getTilesForMetricArea(
        minX: Double,
        maxX: Double,
        minY: Double,
        maxY: Double,
        zoom: Int = 10
    ): List<MetricTileCorners> {
        val effectiveMinX = minX.coerceIn(0.0, 450000.0)
        val effectiveMaxX = maxX.coerceIn(0.0, 450000.0)
        val effectiveMinY = minY.coerceIn(0.0, 500000.0)
        val effectiveMaxY = maxY.coerceIn(0.0, 500000.0)

        val swLatLon = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(Location(effectiveMinX, effectiveMinY))
        val neLatLon = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(Location(effectiveMaxX, effectiveMaxY))

        val minLat = minOf(swLatLon.first, neLatLon.first)
        val maxLat = maxOf(swLatLon.first, neLatLon.first)
        val minLon = minOf(swLatLon.second, neLatLon.second)
        val maxLon = maxOf(swLatLon.second, neLatLon.second)

        val minTileX = lonToTileX(minLon, zoom)
        val maxTileX = lonToTileX(maxLon, zoom)
        val minTileY = latToTileY(maxLat, zoom) // Higher latitude is smaller Y index
        val maxTileY = latToTileY(minLat, zoom)

        val tiles = LinkedHashSet<MetricTileCorners>()
        for (tx in minTileX..maxTileX) {
            for (ty in minTileY..maxTileY) {
                tiles.add(tileToMetricCorners(zoom, tx, ty))
            }
        }

        val cx = (minX + maxX) / 2.0
        val cy = (minY + maxY) / 2.0

        return tiles.sortedBy { tile ->
            val tcx = (tile.minX + tile.maxX) / 2.0
            val tcy = (tile.minY + tile.maxY) / 2.0
            kotlin.math.hypot(tcx - cx, tcy - cy)
        }
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/RouteParser.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import com.example.irishhike.model.Waypoint
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.StringReader

object RouteParser {
    /**
     * Queries OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME via context.contentResolver when given a content:// URI.
     * Extracts the display name and strips the extension (e.g. "glounthaune.csv" -> "glounthaune").
     */
    fun extractRouteNameFromUri(context: Context, uri: Uri, fallbackName: String? = null): String {
        var displayName: String? = null
        if (uri.scheme == "content") {
            try {
                context.contentResolver.query(
                    uri,
                    arrayOf(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME),
                    null,
                    null,
                    null
                )?.use { cursor ->
                    if (cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                        val columnIndex = cursor.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)
                        if (columnIndex != -1) {
                            displayName = cursor.getString(columnIndex)
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Fallback to uri.lastPathSegment or fallbackName if query fails
            }
        }
        if (displayName.isNullOrBlank()) {
            displayName = fallbackName ?: uri.lastPathSegment
        }
        if (displayName.isNullOrBlank()) {
            displayName = "Imported Route"
        }
        return displayName.substringBeforeLast(".")
    }

    fun extractRouteNameFromUri(context: Context, uriString: String, fallbackName: String? = null): String {
        return try {
            val uri = Uri.parse(uriString)
            extractRouteNameFromUri(context, uri, fallbackName)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            (fallbackName ?: uriString.substringAfterLast("/")).substringBeforeLast(".")
        }
    }

    /**
     * Parses CSV route content into a list of Waypoints.
     */
    fun parseCsv(csvContent: String): List<Waypoint> {
        val waypoints = mutableListOf<Waypoint>()
        val reader = BufferedReader(StringReader(csvContent))
        val lines = reader.readLines()
        if (lines.isEmpty()) return waypoints

        val headerLine = lines[0].trim()
        val headers = parseCsvLine(headerLine).map { it.lowercase().trim() }

        val nameIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("name", "title") }
        val gridIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("grid_ref", "grid", "location") }
        val latIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("latitude", "lat") }
        val lonIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("longitude", "lon") }
        val altIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("altitude", "alt", "elevation", "ele") }
        val descIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("description", "desc", "waypoint_desc", "waypoint_description", "notes", "comments") }
        val legDescIdx = headers.indexOfFirst { it in listOf("leg_description", "leg_desc", "leg_notes", "leg_comments") }

        for (i in 1 until lines.size) {
            val line = lines[i].trim()
            if (line.isEmpty() || line.startsWith("#")) continue

            val tokens = parseCsvLine(line)
            if (tokens.isEmpty()) continue

            val name = if (nameIdx >= 0 && nameIdx < tokens.size) {
                tokens[nameIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"")
            } else {
                "Waypoint $i"
            }

            val alt = if (altIdx >= 0 && altIdx < tokens.size) {
                tokens[altIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"").toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0
            } else {
                0.0
            }

            val description = if (descIdx >= 0 && descIdx < tokens.size) {
                tokens[descIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"")
            } else {
                ""
            }

            val legDescription = if (legDescIdx >= 0 && legDescIdx < tokens.size) {
                tokens[legDescIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"")
            } else {
                ""
            }

            val waypoint = if (gridIdx >= 0 && gridIdx < tokens.size && tokens[gridIdx].isNotBlank()) {
                val gridRef = tokens[gridIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"")
                val loc = CoordinateUtils.parseGridRefToLocation(gridRef, alt)
                Waypoint(
                    x = loc.x,
                    y = loc.y,
                    altitude = alt,
                    name = name,
                    description = description,
                    legDescription = legDescription
                )
            } else if (latIdx >= 0 && lonIdx >= 0 && latIdx < tokens.size && lonIdx < tokens.size) {
                val lat = tokens[latIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"").toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0
                val lon = tokens[lonIdx].trim().removeSurrounding("\"", "\"").toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0
                Waypoint.fromLatLon(
                    name = name,
                    lat = lat,
                    lon = lon,
                    altitude = alt,
                    description = description,
                    legDescription = legDescription
                )
            } else {
                continue
            }

            waypoints.add(waypoint)
        }

        return waypoints
    }

    fun generateSampleIrishGridCsv(): String {
        return """
            name,grid_ref,altitude,leg_description
            Start,V860870,120.0,Heading north toward Colt
            Colt,V875882,450.0,Ascend ridge toward Summit
            Summit,V890895,850.0,Follow ridge line down
            Ridge,V905910,650.0,Descend to finish point
            Finish,V920925,100.0,Route complete
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    private fun parseCsvLine(line: String): List<String> {
        val tokens = mutableListOf<String>()
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        var inQuotes = false
        for (c in line) {
            when {
                c == '"' -> inQuotes = !inQuotes
                c == ',' && !inQuotes -> {
                    tokens.add(sb.toString())
                    sb.setLength(0)
                }
                else -> sb.append(c)
            }
        }
        tokens.add(sb.toString())
        return tokens
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/GpsValidationUtils.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.ToneGenerator
import kotlin.math.hypot

data class GpsValidationConfig(
    val modeName: String = "Hiking",
    val minLat: Double = 51.0,
    val maxLat: Double = 56.0,
    val minLon: Double = -11.0,
    val maxLon: Double = -5.0,
    val notifySpeedKmh: Double = 10.0,
    val rejectSpeedKmh: Double = 200.0,
    val maxGpsAccuracyMeters: Float = 50.0f
) {
    companion object {
        val HIKING = GpsValidationConfig(
            modeName = "Hiking",
            notifySpeedKmh = 10.0,
            rejectSpeedKmh = 150.0,
            maxGpsAccuracyMeters = 300.0f
        )
        val CYCLING = GpsValidationConfig(
            modeName = "Cycling",
            notifySpeedKmh = 35.0,
            rejectSpeedKmh = 120.0,
            maxGpsAccuracyMeters = 50.0f
        )
        val DRIVING = GpsValidationConfig(
            modeName = "Driving",
            notifySpeedKmh = 130.0,
            rejectSpeedKmh = 250.0,
            maxGpsAccuracyMeters = 100.0f
        )
    }
}

enum class GpsValidationResult {
    VALID,
    REJECT_NULL_ISLAND,
    REJECT_OUT_OF_BOUNDS,
    REJECT_ACCURACY,
    REJECT_IMPOSSIBLE_SPEED,
    WARN_HIGH_SPEED
}

object GpsValidationUtils {
    var config: GpsValidationConfig = GpsValidationConfig.HIKING

    private var lastValidLat: Double = 0.0
    var lastValidLon: Double = 0.0
    private var lastValidTimestampMs: Long = 0L

    fun reset() {
        lastValidLat = 0.0
        lastValidLon = 0.0
        lastValidTimestampMs = 0L
    }

    fun validateFix(lat: Double, lon: Double, accuracyMeters: Float, timestampMs: Long): GpsValidationResult {
        if (lat == 0.0 && lon == 0.0) {
            return GpsValidationResult.REJECT_NULL_ISLAND
        }

        if (lat !in config.minLat..config.maxLat || lon !in config.minLon..config.maxLon) {
            return GpsValidationResult.REJECT_OUT_OF_BOUNDS
        }

        if (accuracyMeters > config.maxGpsAccuracyMeters) {
            return GpsValidationResult.REJECT_ACCURACY
        }

        if (lastValidTimestampMs > 0 && timestampMs > lastValidTimestampMs) {
            val prevLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(lastValidLat, lastValidLon)
            val currLoc = CoordinateUtils.latLonToMetric(lat, lon)
            val distMeters = prevLoc.distanceTo(currLoc)
            val dtSec = (timestampMs - lastValidTimestampMs) / 1000.0
            if (dtSec > 0.0) {
                val speedMps = distMeters / dtSec
                val speedKmh = speedMps * 3.6

                if (speedKmh > config.rejectSpeedKmh) {
                    return GpsValidationResult.REJECT_IMPOSSIBLE_SPEED
                }
                if (speedKmh > config.notifySpeedKmh) {
                    lastValidLat = lat
                    lastValidLon = lon
                    lastValidTimestampMs = timestampMs
                    return GpsValidationResult.WARN_HIGH_SPEED
                }
            }
        }

        lastValidLat = lat
        lastValidLon = lon
        lastValidTimestampMs = timestampMs
        return GpsValidationResult.VALID
    }

    fun playBeep() {
        try {
            val toneGen = ToneGenerator(AudioManager.STREAM_NOTIFICATION, 80)
            toneGen.startTone(ToneGenerator.TONE_PROP_NACK, 250)
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }
}


// --- FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/irishhike/util/HikerTrackExporter.kt ---

package com.example.irishhike.util

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Environment
import android.util.Log
import android.widget.Toast
import com.example.irishhike.model.Breadcrumb
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.sqrt

object HikerTrackExporter {

    private const val TAG = "HikerTrackExporter"

    fun exportBreadcrumbsToGpx(
        context: Context,
        breadcrumbs: List<Breadcrumb>,
        routeUri: Uri? = null
    ): File? {
        if (breadcrumbs.isEmpty()) return null

        val targetDir = resolveTargetDirectory(context, routeUri)
        if (!targetDir.exists()) {
            targetDir.mkdirs()
        }

        val timeFormatter = SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd-HHmm", Locale.US)
        val timestampStr = timeFormatter.format(Date())
        val filename = "bc-$timestampStr.gpx"
        val file = File(targetDir, filename)

        val isoFormatter = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss'Z'", Locale.US)
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\"?>\n")
        sb.append("<gpx version=\"1.1\" creator=\"Irish Hiker Navigation App\"\n")
        sb.append("  xmlns=\"http://www.topografix.com/GPX/1/1\">\n")
        sb.append("  <trk>\n")
        sb.append("    <name>Hike Breadcrumbs $timestampStr</name>\n")
        sb.append("    <trkseg>\n")

        for (bc in breadcrumbs) {
            val (lat, lon) = CoordinateUtils.metricToLatLon(bc.location)
            val timeIso = isoFormatter.format(Date(bc.timestampMs))
            val accuracy = if (bc.ekfState != null) {
                sqrt(abs(bc.ekfState.position.variance))
            } else {
                if (bc.isGpsFix) 5.0 else 2.0
            }
            val stride = bc.ekfState?.strideLengthMeters?.value ?: 0.75

            sb.append("      <trkpt lat=\"%.6f\" lon=\"%.6f\">\n".format(Locale.US, lat, lon))
            sb.append("        <ele>%.1f</ele>\n".format(Locale.US, bc.location.altitude))
            sb.append("        <time>$timeIso</time>\n")
            sb.append("        <extensions>\n")
            sb.append("          <accuracy>%.2f</accuracy>\n".format(Locale.US, accuracy))
            sb.append("          <stride>%.2f</stride>\n".format(Locale.US, stride))
            sb.append("        </extensions>\n")
            sb.append("      </trkpt>\n")
        }

        sb.append("    </trkseg>\n")
        sb.append("  </trk>\n")
        sb.append("</gpx>\n")

        return try {
            file.writeText(sb.toString())
            Log.i(TAG, "Successfully exported breadcrumbs to: ${file.absolutePath}")
            val displayPath = getRelativePathForDisplay(file)
            Toast.makeText(context, "Saved breadcrumbs to:\n$displayPath", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            file
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to save breadcrumbs: ${e.message}", e)
            Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to save breadcrumbs: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            null
        }
    }

    private fun getRelativePathForDisplay(file: File): String {
        val absPath = file.absolutePath
        val idx = absPath.indexOf("Download")
        return if (idx != -1) absPath.substring(idx) else absPath
    }

    fun resolveTargetDirectory(context: Context, routeUri: Uri?): File {
        if (routeUri != null) {
            val uriString = routeUri.toString()
            Log.d(TAG, "Resolving target directory for routeUri: $uriString")

            if (routeUri.scheme == "file") {
                val file = File(routeUri.path ?: "")
                file.parentFile?.let {
                    Log.d(TAG, "Resolved parent dir from file scheme: ${it.absolutePath}")
                    return it
                }
            }

            val decoded = Uri.decode(uriString)
            val pathPart = when {
                decoded.contains("primary:") -> decoded.substringAfter("primary:")
                decoded.contains("raw:") -> decoded.substringAfter("raw:")
                decoded.contains("/storage/") -> decoded.substringAfter("/storage/")
                else -> null
            }
            if (pathPart != null) {
                val fullPath = if (pathPart.startsWith("/")) pathPart else "/storage/emulated/0/$pathPart"
                val file = File(fullPath)
                file.parentFile?.let { parent ->
                    if (!parent.exists()) parent.mkdirs()
                    Log.d(TAG, "Resolved parent dir from pathPart: ${parent.absolutePath}")
                    return parent
                }
            }

            try {
                context.contentResolver.query(routeUri, arrayOf(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.DATA), null, null, null)?.use { cursor ->
                    if (cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                        val colIdx = cursor.getColumnIndex(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.DATA)
                        if (colIdx != -1) {
                            val dataPath = cursor.getString(colIdx)
                            if (!dataPath.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                val file = File(dataPath)
                                file.parentFile?.let {
                                    Log.d(TAG, "Resolved parent dir via MediaColumns.DATA: ${it.absolutePath}")
                                    return it
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Could not query MediaColumns.DATA: ${e.message}")
            }
        }

        val fallback = File(Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS), "freehike")
        if (!fallback.exists()) fallback.mkdirs()
        Log.d(TAG, "Fallback to freehike dir: ${fallback.absolutePath}")
        return fallback
    }
}
